# Code map

Describes `main` at `92d0f13` (2026-11-13). The file tables, Tests, the control verbs, the flags and the Planned table were each checked against it on 2026-11-16, and the `app.rs` line ranges against `5239006` on 2026-11-16; the Deeper notes were last checked at `11e49a5`. Rewritten 2026-11-11 from the tree, file by file, and brought up to date on 2026-11-12 and 2026-11-16. What is built and what remains per v1 item: [ROADMAP.md](../ROADMAP.md). To find the code for a roadmap step, start at the Index by v1 item below. Update this file in the same pull request as any change that adds, removes, renames or rewires a file.

**Status words:** *present, wired* (on `main` and reached from the app or a binary); *present, core only* (on `main` with tests, but nothing in the app calls it yet); *present, out of v1* (on `main`, belongs to an after-1.0 feature); *stub* (the body is `todo!()` or `unimplemented!()`); *dead code* (no caller and no test).

**Counts:** 536 files and folders described, 332 in the file tables and 204 in Tests (a test file counts as present, wired unless its row says otherwise): 375 present, wired, 117 present, core only, 43 present, out of v1, 0 stub, 1 dead code.

## Contents

- Index by v1 item
- Workspace
- Data flow
- Files by area
- `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` by line range
- Tests
- Control verbs
- Command-line flags and environment variables
- Deeper notes
- Mac, PC and Linux
- Planned, not on main

## Workspace

The root `Cargo.toml` is one workspace (resolver 3, edition 2024, `rust-version` 1.95, licence MIT OR Apache-2.0). It has six members and builds seven binaries.

| Member | Package | Kind | What it holds |
|---|---|---|---|
| `crates/core` | `wavcraft-core` | library | Document, store, file reading and saving, effects, analysis, multitrack model. No UI. |
| `crates/automation` | `wavcraft-automation` | library | `Request`, `Backend`, `Headless`, `Remote`, and the MCP handler `mcp::handle`, which the `mcp` subcommand in `main.rs` calls (N3.5). Its dependencies are `wavcraft-core` and `serde_json` (MIT OR Apache-2.0, workspace pin). |
| `crates/testkit` | `wavcraft-testkit` | library and 6 binaries | Test file generator and the benches. |
| `crates/craft-log` | `craft-log` | library | Logging and the system report. |
| `crates/craft-stage` | `craft-stage` | library | No dependencies. Used by `craft-log`. |
| `apps/wavcraft` | `wavcraft` | 1 binary | The egui app, and the `convert` subcommand with no window. |

The seven binaries:

- `wavcraft` (`apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs`): the window, or with `convert <in> <out>` a conversion with no window, or with `mcp` (N3.5) an MCP server on standard input and output with no window. `cli::parse` (`apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs`) also reads `run <script> <file> [-o <out>]`, which `main` runs on `Headless` (N2.4), and `inspect <file>`, which prints the file's Properties rows and exits 0 (N2.3).
- `wavcraft-testkit` (`crates/testkit/src/main.rs`)
- `wavcraft-bench`, `wavcraft-store-bench`, `wavcraft-edit-bench`, `wavcraft-rack-bench`, `wavcraft-nr-bench` (`crates/testkit/src/bin/<name>/main.rs`)

### Lint levels

The workspace sets `unsafe_code = "forbid"` and warns on clippy `dbg_macro` and `todo`.

| Crate | `unsafe_code` | Where unsafe is allowed |
|---|---|---|
| `wavcraft-core` | forbid (inherited) | nowhere |
| `wavcraft-automation` | forbid (inherited) | nowhere |
| `craft-log` | forbid (inherited) | nowhere |
| `craft-stage` | forbid (inherited) | nowhere |
| `wavcraft-testkit` | deny in `Cargo.toml`. `src/lib.rs` and `src/main.rs` forbid it for themselves. | one `#[allow(unsafe_code)]` in `src/bin/wavcraft-bench/memory.rs` (`GetProcessMemoryInfo`, Windows only) |
| `wavcraft` (app) | deny | `apps/wavcraft/src/win_acl.rs` (`#![allow(unsafe_code)]`, Windows only). Two test helpers that set an environment variable also carry `#[allow(unsafe_code)]`: `issue377_tests.rs` and `f48_device_change_tests.rs`. |

`apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs` also denies clippy `unwrap_used`, `expect_used`, `panic`, `unimplemented`, `todo` and `unreachable`. `crates/core/src/lib.rs` and the testkit library, binary and benches carry the same deny list. The dev profile builds my code at opt-level 1 and dependencies at opt-level 3.

### Dependency graph

```
wavcraft (app)
  -> wavcraft-core
  -> wavcraft-automation    (Request::parse in app.rs, Headless for convert in main.rs)
  -> craft-log -> craft-stage
  -> soundcraft-playback, -model, -mix, -audio-io, -dsp, -video
  -> eframe, egui, cpal, rfd, arboard, dirs, serde_json, log
  -> windows-sys            (Windows only)

wavcraft-core
  -> soundcraft-audio-io, -dsp, -model, -time, -mix, -vst3-host
  -> craft-mp3              (pinned, no caller yet)
  -> symphonia, vorbis_rs, rustfft, rayon, bytemuck
  -> dirs, sysinfo, getrandom, serde, postcard, serde_json, log
  -> rustix                 (unix only)

wavcraft-automation -> wavcraft-core, serde_json
wavcraft-testkit    -> wavcraft-core, soundcraft-audio-io, rayon
                    -> windows-sys   (Windows only)
```

In the app, `control_request` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs`) parses OPEN, SELECT, ACTION, EFFECT with a name and SAVE with `wavcraft_automation::Request::parse` (N1.6). It keeps its own arms for a bare EFFECT (the list), `ACTION window.*` and STATE. `convert` in `main.rs` runs `Request::Open` and then `Request::Save` on a `Headless` backend. The one app path that uses `Remote` is `mcp --attach` (N3.5): `serve_mcp` in `main.rs`. `mcp::handle` is answered in the same file by `serve`, for `mcp` and `mcp --attach`.

### The soundcraft pin

All soundcraft crates come from one git revision of the soundcraft repo: `1435fd2874ed7d668cfe0b2f1ef884bce045dd91` (version 0.13.1 in `Cargo.lock`), since `be5a8d8` (#1277). The comment in the root `Cargo.toml` names it the backport tag `wavcraft-pin/e3c5c67-true-peak`: soundcraft `e3c5c67` plus the true-peak fix in `meter.rs` from `d293336`, so a true peak no longer drops a sample at a chunk seam. The root `Cargo.toml` names eight crates at that revision: `soundcraft-audio-io`, `soundcraft-dsp`, `soundcraft-video`, `soundcraft-model`, `soundcraft-time`, `soundcraft-mix`, `soundcraft-playback`, `soundcraft-vst3-host`. `Cargo.lock` also holds `soundcraft-clap-host`, `soundcraft-au-host` and `soundcraft-midi`. `soundcraft-clap-host` and `soundcraft-au-host` come in through `soundcraft-mix`. `soundcraft-midi` comes in through `soundcraft-mix` and `soundcraft-model`. They compile, but my code loads nothing from them.

What I use each one for:

- `soundcraft-audio-io`: `detect_format` in `stream_file` (`crates/core/src/io.rs`) and in `crates/core/src/probe.rs`, the shared types (`AudioBuffer`, `AudioInfo`, `FileFormat`, `SampleFormat`, re-exported from `crates/core/src/lib.rs`; `BwfInfo` is used but not re-exported), `probe` in `multitrack.rs`, and `encode` and `decode` for whole buffers (`document.rs`, `multitrack.rs`; `session_file.rs` uses `encode` only, `mtrecord.rs` uses `decode` only) and the testkit.
- `soundcraft-dsp`: filters and processors for effects (`biquad` in `effects/eq.rs` and `deesser.rs`, `create` and `Plugin` in `effects/dsp_run.rs`, `PitchShifter` and `Saturator` in `effects/pitch_saturate.rs`, `FadeShape` and gain helpers in `effects/oneshot.rs`), the `Plugin` trait in `crates/core/src/vst3.rs`, `pitch_detect` in `timepitch.rs`, `LoudnessMeter` in `loudness.rs`, `PeakMeter` in `levels.rs`, and the pan law in `apps/wavcraft/src/mixer.rs`.
- `soundcraft-model`: the multitrack session types (`Session`, `Track`, `Clip`, the id types, `SampleSource`) in `crates/core/src/multitrack.rs`, `multitrack/`, `sc_source.rs`, `session_file.rs`, `mixdown.rs`, `mtplay.rs`, `mtrecord.rs`, `mtpunch.rs`, and in the app `multitrack_ui.rs`, `mixer.rs` and `app.rs`.
- `soundcraft-time`: `TempoMap`, `BarBeat` and `SampleRate` in `crates/core/src/tempo.rs` and `sc_source.rs`.
- `soundcraft-mix`: `MixEngine`, `ReadAheadSource` and `render_range` for multitrack playback (`crates/core/src/mtplay.rs`), `RangeRender` for mixdown (`mixdown.rs`), and the app mixer (`apps/wavcraft/src/mixer.rs`).
- `soundcraft-playback`: `Player` as the output device for multitrack only (`apps/wavcraft/src/multitrack_ui.rs`). The single-file editor talks to cpal directly in `apps/wavcraft/src/player.rs`.
- `soundcraft-video`: `Movie` and `VideoError` in `apps/wavcraft/src/video.rs`.
- `soundcraft-vst3-host`: plugin scan and load in `crates/core/src/vst3.rs` (`default_search_paths`, `add_search_dir`, `find_bundles`, `scan_paths`, `instantiate_plugin`, `Vst3Plugin::reactivate`).

### Other external crates

- `symphonia` 0.5.5, MPL-2.0. I use it unmodified to decode MP3, FLAC, Vorbis, AAC, ALAC, ADPCM, MP4, CAF and MKV (`open_symphonia` in `crates/core/src/io.rs`), and the WAV, AIFF and CAF files whose codec `open_wav`, `open_aiff` and `containers::open_caf` hand over. `Cargo.lock` holds two versions: 0.5.5 (mine, for `wavcraft-core`) and 0.6.1 (through `soundcraft-audio-io`). The comment above `symphonia` in the root `Cargo.toml` still says both are the same version and features; that is out of date.
- `craft-mp3` 1.1.0, MIT OR Apache-2.0, a git dependency pinned by `rev` to commit `456f049`, the commit the `v1.1.0` tag of the craft-mp3 repo names (A3.1, #1310; moved up from 1.0.0 in `87b10c6`). 1.1.0 adds gapless playback: the encoder delay and padding in the Info tag (craft-mp3 #25 and #27). The comment in the root `Cargo.toml` says the API is the same as 1.0.0. A pure-Rust MP3 encoder with no dependencies, shared with SoundCraft and listed in `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt`. Only `crates/core/Cargo.toml` depends on it, and no code calls `craft_mp3` yet (A3.4 is the writer).
- `vorbis_rs` 0.5.6, BSD-3-Clause. It vendors libvorbis and libogg as C source (`aotuv_lancer_vorbis_sys` and `ogg_next_sys`, both built with `cc`). Every build needs a C compiler.
- `eframe` and `egui` 0.36 with features `wgpu`, `wayland`, `x11` and `default_fonts`. The bundled fonts are Hack (MIT and Bitstream Vera), Ubuntu Light (Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0), Noto Emoji (OFL 1.1) and emoji-icon-font (MIT). `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt` lists them. `tools/notices.py` makes that file.
- `cpal` 0.18 for audio devices. `rfd` 0.17 for file dialogs (XDG portal on Linux). `arboard` 3.6 for the clipboard, default features off.
- `rustfft`, `rayon`, `bytemuck` for analysis and the store. `serde` and `postcard` for peak cache files. `sysinfo` and `rustix` for free disk space. `dirs` for the config, cache and audio folders. `getrandom` for temp file names.
- `wgpu` 30 and `image` 0.25 arrive through `eframe` and `soundcraft-video`.

## Data flow

**Open.** `WaveApp::open` refuses while a take is recording or going into the file, then calls `open_as` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs`). `open_as` cancels an open, effect job or insert that is still running, and starts a thread with a new cancel flag. The thread calls `Document::open_in` (or `open_raw_in`) with `OpenOptions` and that flag (`crates/core/src/document.rs`). `open_in` takes a `peakcache::Key::of_file`, then `io::open` runs `open_inner`: `Store::create_session`, then `stream_file`. `stream_file` picks `open_wav`, `open_aiff`, or `containers::open_w64`, `open_caf`, `open_au`, `open_raw`. Anything else goes through `detect_format` and `open_symphonia`. Samples land in the on-disk `Store` in blocks of 65,536 frames. `space::free_space` checks the disk first. The result is a `DocState`. `Document::from_state_cached` then loads peaks with `peakcache::load`, or builds them with `build_all` (one thread per channel) and saves them with `peakcache::store`. `finish_opening` polls the thread, parks the old document, calls `Player::set_audio`, makes a new `Editor` over a new `History`, and bumps `generation`. Cancel in the status bar and the `OPEN_CANCEL` control verb call `cancel_opening`, which sets the flag and drops the files queued behind it (`open_queue`). `io::open` then stops, removes the store and its folder, and returns `OpenError::Cancelled`, so no document is added (A1.1, A1.2, A1.6).

**Edit and history.** The edit functions in `crates/core/src/doc/edit.rs` (`delete`, `cut`, `paste`, `silence`, `crop`, `replace_range`, `generate`, `mix_paste`) return an `Edit` with a new `DocState`. They change the piece table, not the samples. `Editor::run` and `Editor::run_effect` (`crates/core/src/actions.rs`) push the result with `History::push_edit`. Long effects run in `jobs::Job::spawn_replace` and push in `finish_job`. Undo, redo and `go_to` hand back an earlier `Arc<DocState>`. After any step `WaveApp::rebuild` starts `Document::after_step`. That calls `EditSpan::between` (`doc/span.rs`) to find what changed, then `WavePeaks::updated` per channel with the `PeakBlockCache` (256-frame summaries keyed by block, in `crates/core/src/document.rs`). It falls back to `build_all` when the change covers more than half the document. `finish_rebuild` calls `Player::replace_audio` and bumps `generation`. `Recovery::track` (`apps/wavcraft/src/recovery.rs`) hands a `Snapshot` to the `Autosaver`, which writes it with `write_journal`. A transcript rides on the `DocState` (`DocState::transcript`). `WaveApp::finish_opening` loads `<file>.transcript.json` with `transcript::load_beside` into the first state (a bad sidecar is logged and skipped), and `WaveApp::finish_saving` writes it with `transcript::save_beside` when the saved state has a transcript (L1.12). `History::push_transcript` adds one as its own step. `finish` in `doc/edit.rs` keeps it only for an edit that moves no audio; any other edit drops it until L3.3, and undo brings it back.

**Spectrogram worker.** `Renderer::new` (`apps/wavcraft/src/spectrum.rs`) starts one worker thread. Each frame the app calls `Renderer::ask` with a `Job` (generation, columns, rows, parameters). The worker keeps only the newest request. It reuses an `Overview` when `Overviews::serve` finds one for the same FFT size, window and dB range. Otherwise it builds one with `Overview::build_from`. On a new generation it calls `Overview::after_step`, which uses `spectral_dirty::dirty_blocks` to redo only the changed blocks (16 hops each), then `refresh_visible`. It keeps one retired overview so undo straight back is instant. It draws with `spectrogram::render_columns_from`, colours the result into an `egui::ColorImage`, and the app collects it with `Renderer::take`.

**Playback.** `Player::new` (`apps/wavcraft/src/player.rs`) starts the `wavcraft-readahead` thread, which runs `read_ahead`. `Player::set_audio` sends it the document. `Player::start` and `Player::play` call `begin_play`. The thread then runs `begin`, and `top_up` and `fill_all` read from `DocState::read` into a ring (`RING_FRAMES` is 131,072 frames, `RING_CHANNELS` is 8). `open_stream` and `build` open the cpal device. `device_callback` calls `render`, then `render_with`. That path never blocks and never allocates. It plays silence and counts an underrun when the ring is short. `rebuild_resampler` sets up `wavcraft_core::resample` when the device rate differs. `watch_default_device` and `Player::maintain` reopen the stream when the default device changes. With `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO` set, `Sink` stands in for the device. Multitrack playback is separate: `PlaybackSession` in `crates/core/src/mtplay.rs` with `soundcraft_playback::Player`.

**Recording.** File › New Recording…, the Record button and File › Record Now (Shift+Space, `quick_record`) start from `Recording::start_format(Recording::device_channels())`: the last format used, else `rec_input::first_format` with the microphone's channel count from `rec_input::default_channels` (48 kHz, 24-bit; mono with no device or with the fake input). `device_channels` returns `None` when `FakeSpec::from_env` finds a fake input (J1.5). `WaveApp::start_recording` leads to `Recording::start_with` (`apps/wavcraft/src/recording.rs`). `record_dir(preference)` picks the folder: `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR`, else the `recordings_folder` setting (held in `Recording::recordings_folder`, set by the app before each start and dialog), else `WavCraft Recordings` in the audio, documents or home folder; the pure `record_dir_from` holds that order (`crates/core/src/settings.rs` keeps the setting; J1.2). `record::create_take` creates the WAV file (exclusive create) and returns a `TakeWriter`. `rec_input::open` opens the cpal input stream. `FakeInput::spawn` (`crates/core/src/record.rs`) replaces it when `FakeSpec::from_env` finds a fake input. The device callback calls `Ring::push`, which never blocks. It drops and counts frames when the ring is full. `Recorder::start` runs a writer thread that drains `Ring::pop` into the `TakeWriter`. The writer rewrites and syncs the 44-byte header once a second (`HEADER_INTERVAL`), so a crash loses at most a second. `Recording::meter` gives the status bar the newest peak and a clip flag that stays on until the take ends; `WaveApp::meter_peaks` and `sync_input` (app.rs) read it through `bars_source`, so the Levels bars show the take while recording. An optional `MonitorRing` feeds `Player::set_monitor` (`attach_monitor`). `finish_recording` calls `Recording::stop`, which returns a `Take`. The app then opens the take with `WaveApp::open`, or runs `integrate_take` (`io::open` plus `rec_edit::apply_take_with_offset`) and `finish_integration`, which pushes one edit.

**Save.** `WaveApp::save` calls `save_with`. It picks the path (an `rfd` dialog when needed), the `SaveFormat` from the extension (`Wav`, `Aiff`, `Flac`, `Vorbis`, `Mp3`), or a separate `Container` value for W64, CAF and AU, and the `SampleType`. A thread calls `save_wav_with`, `save_aiff_kind` (AIFF and FLAC), `save_vorbis` or `save_container`. `save_container` is the app's name for `save_as` in `crates/core/src/save.rs`. In `crates/core/src/save.rs`, `save_inner` removes old temp files with `remove_leftover_temps`, makes a `TempFile` beside the target (`TempFile::create`, exclusive create), and runs `write_stream`. `write_stream` reads 64 K frames at a time through `DocState::read`. For WAV it copies the source's extra chunks (`plan_chunks`) and switches to RF64 above 4 GiB. FLAC uses my own encoder in `flac_enc.rs`. Vorbis uses `write_vorbis_stream`. The last step is `std::fs::rename` over the target. `finish_saving` updates the saved marker and path. `save_copy` does the same for Save Selection As and Export. `Mp3` is written by `save_mp3` (A3.4) or by `save` at the default options (192 kbps), through the craft-mp3 encoder, and the encoded stream is held in memory until the save ends. `save_inner` runs `mp3_check` (`crates/core/src/mp3_options.rs`, A3.2) before any file is made, so a rate or channel count MP3 cannot hold is refused with `SaveError::Unsupported` and leaves no file; with `mono` set, a stereo document is mixed down to one channel (A3.5, #1464). In the window `save_with` sends a `.mp3` name to `save_mp3` with `Mp3Options` made from `mp3_bitrate` and `mp3_mono` (192 kbps stereo until changed); the `SAVE` verb sets them from `bitrate=` and `channels=` (A3.6), and the Save As dialog has no MP3 choices yet (A3.7). `Headless` writes MP3 the same way (A3.11): `save_mp3` with the `bitrate=` and `channels=` of the `SAVE` line, kept for later saves, so `wavcraft convert in.wav out.mp3` writes 192 kbps stereo. The Save As filters do not offer MP3. Its size estimate is at constant bit rate: `estimate_size` calls `estimate_mp3_size` (A3.10) with 192 kbps, and the Save As size line says so.

**Convert.** `main` (`apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs`) first checks for the VST3 scan worker flag, then reads the arguments with `cli::parse`. For `convert <in> <out>` it calls `convert` before `selflog::install` and before any window. `convert` makes a `Headless` (`crates/automation/src/headless.rs`) over a session folder (`--session-dir`, else `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR`, else `wavcraft-sessions` in the system temp folder) and runs `Request::Open`, then `Request::Save` with default `SaveArgs`. So the output's extension picks the format and the source's sample type is kept where the format holds it, as in the window's Save As. Exit code 0 means saved. Otherwise the reason goes to standard error and the exit code is 2. `apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs` checks that no session folder is left behind either way.

**Episode Check and Transcribe.** Both work like the Sample Statistics scan (`statspanel.rs`). `run_action` in `app.rs` hands the current `Arc<DocState>` to `CheckRun::start` (`apps/wavcraft/src/check_panel.rs`) for `check.run`, or to `TranscriptRun::start` (`apps/wavcraft/src/transcript_run.rs`) for `transcript.run`. A new start cancels a running one. Each starts a named thread (`episode check`, `transcribe`) that streams the document from the store, with a cancel flag and progress in thousandths: `check::scan_doc`, or `transcript::transcribe` with `FakeEngine` when `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH=1` (with no engine, the run fails). Every frame the app polls both (`CheckRun::poll` in `WaveApp::ui`, `poll_transcript` in `WaveApp::logic`) and draws progress and Cancel in the status bar. A finished check keeps its `Report` with the state it scanned (`Checked`); once the history moves on, the status bar says the report is stale. A finished transcript is pushed with `History::push_transcript` as the step "Transcribe", but only when the document has not changed meanwhile. The control verbs are `CHECK run`, `cancel` and `state`, and the same three for `TRANSCRIPT`.

### Recording (`crates/core`)

| File | What it owns | Key names | Uses | Status | Items | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `apps/wavcraft/src/rec_input.rs` | Opening the real input device for a take: the device named in the preferences or the system default. Its callback converts to f32 and pushes to the ring without blocking; the caller starts the stream. `first_format` (the default 48 kHz 24-bit format with the mic's channel count, kept to 1 to 8, mono with no device) and `default_channels` (the input device's channel count from its default config) are called by `Recording::device_channels` and `Recording::start_format` (`recording.rs`, J1.5). | `open`, `Opened`, `Lost`, `monitor_frames`, `first_format`, `default_channels` | cpal; wavcraft_core::record (Ring, RecFormat, ChannelPick, MonitorRing); input_monitor::find_input | present, wired | J1, J5 | 2 in-file tests (`the_first_take_has_the_mics_channel_count`, `a_named_input_that_is_gone_falls_back_to_the_default`); crates/core/tests/j5_input_device.rs for the core side |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/recording.rs` | Recording a take in the window: the dialog, start options, timed and armed starts, pause, punch into an open document, the live input (real or fake), monitoring, and the status readout with level, time, size and dropped samples. The readout's time, size, time left and dropped-samples texts come from `take_readout` through `status_numbers`; `left_secs` still gives STATE its `rec_left_s`. `Recording::meter` gives the readout its peak and a clip flag that stays on until the take ends; the Levels bars read it through `bars_source` in app.rs (J4.3), so they show the take while recording. `record_dir(preference)` takes `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR`, else the `recordings_folder` setting (held in `Recording::recordings_folder`, set by the app before each start and dialog), else a `WavCraft Recordings` folder in the audio, documents or home folder; the pure `record_dir_from` holds that order. | `Recording`, `StartOpts`, `parse_start_args`, `record_dir`, `record_dir_from`, `Recording::start_with`, `Recording::meter`, `Recording::device_channels`, `Recording::start_format`, `status_numbers`, `RECORD` | wavcraft_core::record, doc::rec_edit, space, take_readout; rec_input; egui | present, wired | J1, J3, J4, J5, J7 | 14 in-file tests (among them `the_take_meter_follows_the_input_and_latches_clip`, `the_status_numbers_come_from_the_take_readout`, `the_first_take_follows_the_mic_and_later_takes_keep_the_last_format` and `the_recordings_folder_is_env_then_preference_then_default`); crates/core/tests/rec_edit.rs and the in-file tests of crates/core/src/record.rs for the core side |

| File | What it owns | Key names | Uses | Status | Items | Tests |

| `crates/automation/Cargo.toml` | The manifest of the wavcraft-automation library crate; its dependencies are wavcraft-core and serde_json (for the MCP handler). The app takes it as a normal dependency since N1.6. | `wavcraft-automation` | wavcraft-core, serde_json | present, wired | N1, N2 | none |
| `crates/automation/src/headless.rs` | The windowless backend: it opens a file into a session store, keeps one editor, a selection and the file a bare SAVE writes, and answers each request with the same core calls the window makes: the registry effects and the one-shot effects (`actions::ONE_SHOT`: reverse, the fades) by `EFFECT` name, and `SAVE` with the window's options (sample type from bits=/float=, AIFF kind, Vorbis quality, meta=0 through `save_wav_with`) and its format choice (Wave64, CAF and AU through `save_as`; a name without an extension is a WAV; MP3 is written by `save_mp3` with the bit rate and channels from `bitrate=` and `channels=`, kept for later saves as the window keeps them, A3.11). `wavcraft convert` and `wavcraft run` (apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs) run on it. `run_script` runs a script text with `script::run`, squashes the steps it made into one step 'Run Script' on success, and on a refused line goes back to the starting step, deletes the steps after it and restores the selection (N4.4); `step_count` counts history steps. | `Headless`, `Headless::new`, `Headless::with_crossfades`, `Headless::document`, `Headless::run_script`, `Headless::step_count`, `impl Backend for Headless` | wavcraft_core io::open, actions::{Editor, ONE_SHOT}, doc::history::History (`squash_since`), script::run, doc::edit::Crossfades, effects, save (`save_wav_with`, `save_aiff_kind`, `save_vorbis`, `save_mp3`, `save_as`, `SaveError`) | present, wired | A3 (A3.11), N1, N2 (N2.2), N4 (N4.4) | crates/automation/tests/headless.rs; crates/automation/tests/script.rs (`run_script`); apps/wavcraft/src/n1_parity_tests.rs; apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs through `wavcraft convert` and `wavcraft run` |
| `crates/automation/src/lib.rs` | The six control requests as one enum with a parser and a wire spelling, the `SAVE` options (`SaveArgs`: an optional path, then bits=, float=, aiff=, quality=, meta=, bitrate=, channels=; a bare SAVE is valid), and the Backend trait that answers a request or a raw line with OK or ERR text. | `Request`, `Request::parse`, `Request::line`, `SaveArgs`, `SaveArgs::parse` (`SaveArgs` has `bitrate` and `channels` for MP3), `take_bitrate`, `take_channels`, `Backend`, `Backend::answer_line`, verbs `OPEN` `SELECT` `ACTION` `EFFECT` `SAVE` `STATE` | wavcraft_core::save (`SampleType`, `AiffKind`), wavcraft_core::mp3_options (`MP3_BITRATES`); modules headless, mcp, remote, script; app.rs uses it for OPEN, SELECT, ACTION, EFFECT with a name and SAVE, while retaining bare EFFECT and ACTION window.* handlers | present, wired | A3 (A3.6), N1 | crates/automation/tests/request.rs |
| `crates/automation/src/mcp.rs` | The MCP handler: one line of newline-delimited JSON-RPC 2.0 in, at most one line out. It answers `initialize` (echoing the client's protocolVersion, with `capabilities.tools` and `serverInfo.name` wavcraft), `tools/list` (seven tools: open, select, action, effect, state, save, list_effects, each with an object input schema) and `tools/call`: the six request tools become a `Request` run on the Backend, and its reply is text (a backend error is an `isError` result); `list_effects` gives `effects::describe` for each effect. A bad or missing argument is an `isError` result too. A notification gets no reply; bad JSON is -32700 with a null id, an unknown method or tool name -32601 or -32602, and the id is echoed. | `handle`, `call` | serde_json; wavcraft_core effects | present, wired | N3.1, N3.2, N3.4 (the ten tasks through the window: `ten_tasks_through_the_window` in apps/wavcraft/src/n3_mcp_window_tests.rs), N3.5 (`serve_mcp` in main.rs) | crates/automation/tests/mcp.rs |
| `crates/automation/src/remote.rs` | The backend for a running app: it connects to the loopback control port, presents the token first, sends one request per line, and after OPEN and after SAVE polls STATE until busy=0, then asks LASTERROR and returns its text as the error unless it is `-`. | `Remote`, `Remote::connect`, `Remote::from_env`, `impl Backend for Remote`, env `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT`, `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE` | std::net; the wire format of apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs. The example crates/automation/examples/headless_window_parity.rs constructs it with `Remote::from_env`, and so does `serve_mcp` in main.rs for `mcp --attach` (N3.5) | present, wired | N1, N3.5 | crates/automation/tests/remote.rs |
| `crates/automation/src/script.rs` | The script reader and runner: `parse` turns a script text into one `Request` per line with its 1-based line number. Blank lines and lines starting with # are skipped, CRLF is accepted, an OPEN or SAVE line is refused, and the first bad line is reported as `line N: reason`. `run` parses the text (a bad line runs nothing), then executes the lines in order on any `Backend`, stops at the first refused line and returns `line N: reason` (N4.2). | `parse`, `run` | `Request::parse` in lib.rs, `Backend` in lib.rs; used by `Headless::run_script` (N4.4) and by `run_script` and `ScriptBackend` in apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs (the `RUN_SCRIPT` verb, N4.5) | present, wired | N4.1, N4.2, N4.4, N4.5 | crates/automation/tests/script.rs |

### The app: shell (`apps/wavcraft`)

| File | What it owns | Key names | Uses | Status | Items | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `apps/wavcraft/Cargo.toml` | The app crate's manifest: its dependencies on the core and automation crates, the SoundCraft crates, eframe, cpal, rfd, arboard and craft-log, and the lint rule that denies unsafe code outside the Windows ACL file. **Platform:** windows-sys with the Win32 security features is a dependency on Windows only; eframe is built with both wayland and x11 for Linux. | `wavcraft` package, `unsafe_code = "deny"`, `windows-sys` (Windows only) | wavcraft-core, soundcraft-playback, soundcraft-model, soundcraft-mix, soundcraft-audio-io, soundcraft-dsp, soundcraft-video, eframe, egui, cpal, rfd, arboard, dirs, log, craft-log, serde_json; windows-sys on Windows only; wavcraft-automation (a normal dependency since N1.6: `Request::parse` in app.rs and `Headless` in main.rs's `convert`) | present, wired | I5, N1, N2 | none |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` | The whole window as one struct: document slots, open, save, export, editor drawing and gestures, playback, recording glue, panels and docking, menus (the Effects menu and, beside it, the Favorites menu of E1.3), preferences, dialogs, background job polling, and the control channel's verb table. OPEN, SELECT, ACTION, EFFECT with a name, and SAVE use `wavcraft_automation::Request::parse`; bare EFFECT and ACTION window.* remain local. `EFFECT_DIALOG PREVIEW` starts or stops the effect preview worker (E6.2), which stops whenever the dialog or the document changes. `RUN_SCRIPT <path>` runs a script file on the open document through `script::run` and `ScriptBackend`, squashes it to one step 'Run Script' with `History::squash_since`, and on a bad line goes back to the start step and answers `ERR line N: reason` (N4.5). `FRAMETIMES` reports the work time of recent frames, which `frame_timer_begin` (the start of `logic`) and `frame_timer_end` (the end of `ui`) record (B1.3). `save_with` takes the Save As filter list from `v1::save_formats_in`, so a release build offers no Ogg Vorbis filter (M5.3). A `.mp3` name reaches `save_mp3` from `save_with`, with the `mp3_bitrate` and `mp3_mono` the `SAVE` verb sets (A3.6). The Window menu's Mixer panel checkbox and the mixer panel block are drawn only when `v1::offered(Feature::Multitrack)` (M5.4). `Tool` has a `Lasso` variant (name `lasso`, C4.6) and `Tool::selects_time`, which the Lasso shares with Select. The effect dialog's Preview button toggles the live preview, and a changed setting goes to the worker through `set_params` (E6.3). A fade handle drag ends in `run_fade` (E1.2). `WaveApp::edit_picture` (`spectral::EditToPicture`, B2.5) starts in `rebuild`, learns the new generation in `finish_rebuild` and stops when `editor` installs the new `SpectrumCache`; open, close, switch, `add_document` and recovery reset it. `run_window_action` also handles `ViewSpectralToggle` (B2.1, it flips the `spectral` panel) and Repeat Last Command with its dialog (E5.2). `begin_recording` puts the Levels clip lights out when a take starts (J4.4, `Levels::reset_clip`). The Effects menu has Save Noise Print… and Load Noise Print…, and `NOISE_PRINT SAVE <path>` and `NOISE_PRINT LOAD <path>` do the same (G1.2). The Playback and Recording page of Preferences has a Recordings folder row (J1.3). The Save As window has Dithering, Dither Type and Noise Shaping controls (A4.4), and `save_with` passes the choice to `save_dithered`. `SELBOX <start\|end\|duration> <time>` types a time into a Selection box through `apply_panel_box`, the one place a confirmed box is applied (B5). The Noise Reduction dialog's Select Entire File button runs `select_entire_file` (G2.6). **Platform:** `cfg!(target_os = "macos")` picks shortcut text (Cmd for Ctrl) in menus and tool labels. `reveal_label` names the file manager per OS (macOS, Windows, other). No cfg-gated code paths otherwise. | `WaveApp`, `WaveApp::new`, `control_request`, `run_action` (it flips the Match Loudness panel for `ToggleMatchPanel`, G3.4), `run_command` (it opens the Noise Reduction dialog for `Command::NoiseReductionDialog`, G2.4), `open_effect_dialog`, `DocSlot`, `impl eframe::App` (`logic`, `ui`), `bars_source` (J4.3: the Levels bars' peaks, and whether they show the input), `toggle_effect_preview`, `stop_effect_preview`, `save_noise_print`, `load_noise_print`, `noise_print_summary`, `run_script`, `ScriptBackend`, `frame_timer_begin`, `frame_timer_end`, `WaveApp::run_fade`, `draw_fade_handles`, `apply_panel_box`, `WaveApp::select_entire_file`, the fields `fade_drag` and `fade_origin_y`, `Tool::selects_time`, `shortcut_help_rows` (the Help list's rows: the action table in menu order, one per bound chord, from `TABLE` and `Keymap::chords`) and `shortcut_help_content` (two labels per row); It first answers the hidden VST3 scan worker mode (`vst3::WORKER_FLAG`), then parses the command line with `cli::parse`; a parse error exits 2 with the message on standard error. `convert` opens and saves through `Headless` and exits 0, or 2 with the reason, before the log or a window starts; its session stores go in `--session-dir`, else `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR`, else `wavcraft-sessions` in the system temp folder. `mcp` (N3.5) runs before the log and the window: `serve_mcp` connects (`Remote::from_env` for `--attach`, else `Headless`), then `serve` answers each standard-input line through `mcp::handle` and flushes the reply, and exits 0 at the end of input or 2 with the reason. `inspect <file>` opens the file into the session folder, prints its Properties rows (`Key: value` per line) and exits 0, or 2 with the reason, before the log or a window starts (N2.3). `run <script> <file> [-o <out>]` (N2.4) opens the file through `Headless`, runs the script text with `Headless::run_script`, and only when every line passed saves to `<out>` (none given: nothing is saved); exit 0, or 2 with the reason on standard error (a bad line reads `line N: reason`); before the log or a window starts. With no subcommand it installs the log, picks the graphics backend and opens the window. **Platform:** Release builds on Windows use the windows subsystem (no console). On Windows wgpu is limited to DirectX 12 unless WGPU_BACKEND is set. `win_acl` is compiled on Windows only. | `main`, `convert`, `inspect`, `run_script_file`, `serve_mcp`, `serve`, `exit_error`, `conversion_sessions_dir`, `parse_args`, `Args`, `configure_graphics` | app, cli, selflog, every sibling module (mod list); wavcraft_automation::{Headless, Request, SaveArgs, Backend, mcp::handle, remote::Remote} (`Headless::run_script` for `run`); wavcraft_core::vst3; eframe, egui, wgpu | present, wired | A1, I5, N2 (N2.1, N2.2, N2.3, N2.4), N3.5 | 1 in-file test (`the_session_dir_flag_and_the_file_in_any_order`); apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs (15 tests) runs the binary. ### The app: control channel

| File | What it owns | Key names | Uses | Status | Items | Tests |

| `apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs` | The command line as a pure parser. No subcommand is the window, with today's `--session-dir`, `--reset-preferences` and FILE; any other flag is now an error, not a file name. `run <script> [FILE] [-o OUT]`, `convert <in> <out>` and `inspect <file>` take their operands; `mcp [--attach]` takes no operand, only the flag (N3.5); `convert`, `inspect` and `run` also take `--session-dir DIR` or `--session-dir=DIR`, before or after the subcommand (`convert`, `inspect` and `run` use it for their session store; `run` since N2.4). An unknown flag, or a missing or extra operand, is an error whose message names it and gives the usage line. It opens no file and prints nothing; main.rs runs `convert`, `inspect`, `run` and `mcp`. | `Command` (`Window`, `Run`, `Convert`, `Inspect`, `Mcp`), `parse` | crate `Args` and `parse_args` (main.rs) | present, wired | N2 (N2.1, N2.2, N2.3, N2.4), N3.5 | 13 in-file tests (`convert_takes_in_and_out`, `convert_takes_session_dir_in_equals_form_before_the_subcommand`, `a_bad_flag_is_an_error_with_a_message`, `a_bad_flag_with_no_subcommand_is_an_error`, `a_missing_operand_is_an_error`, `no_subcommand_is_the_window`, `run_takes_o`, `inspect_takes_a_file`, `inspect_takes_session_dir_after_the_file`, `inspect_takes_session_dir_before_the_subcommand`, `inspect_with_no_file_is_an_error`, `mcp_takes_attach`, `mcp_refuses_a_bad_flag_and_an_operand`); apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs through the binary |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs` | The loopback control channel: a TCP listener on 127.0.0.1 that checks the token line, forwards each request line to the window thread and writes back the answer. They bind Ctrl+Shift+P (Cmd+Shift+P on a Mac) to `Command::NoiseReductionDialog` (control name `nr.open_dialog`, menu text "Noise Reduction…", G2.4), which is not a row of `actions::TABLE`, and Alt+5 to the Match Loudness panel (G3.4). They bind Spectral Frequency Display on / off to Shift+D and F9 (B2.1). Convert Sample Type is on Shift+T and F11 (A4.1). The four spectral scale keys are on Ctrl+Alt+Up / Down / PageUp / PageDown (B2.3). Plain D is the Lasso Selection Tool (`tool.lasso`, C4.6); Ctrl+D stays Deselect All and Shift+D stays the spectral toggle. **Platform:** No cfg sites. ### Tools (`tools/`)

| File | What it owns | Key names | Uses | Status | Items | Tests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `tools/alpha` | 323 files of scripted feature runs. fNN.py drives the release app through the control channel in a scratch home; fNN.sh builds the app and runs it; -check, -test and -all scripts run fmt, clippy and tests. `k1.py` (K1.6) opens a generated 20 s WAV clipped at 3.0 s, runs `ACTION check.run` until `CHECK state` shows `running=0 progress=100` with at least one finding and `stale=0`, checks `stale=1` after an edit, and cancels a scan of a generated 30-minute WAV within 1 s. `l1.py` (L1.11), with `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH=1`, transcribes a generated 65 s WAV to 3 words (the first at 48000-72000) in one new History step, and cancels a 30-minute run within 1 s with no words and no new step. Both work in a system temp folder they remove, on port 47196 with `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1`. `g4_1.py` (G4.1) reads STATS and LOUDNESS through the control channel for the kit tone, pink and (with `--long`) long files, measures the same files with ffmpeg (`astats` for peak, RMS and DC; `ebur128=peak=true` for integrated loudness and true peak) and checks them within the tolerances at the top of the script; it asserts the clipped count is 0 and does not compare it with ffmpeg. `g4_1.sh` builds the app and runs it on a kit folder. `g35.py` (G3.5) runs MATCH with its default podcast target on a long kit WAV through the control channel (the socket has no read timeout, because the reply comes when the run ends), saves a copy to a scratch folder and measures that copy with ffmpeg `ebur128=peak=true`: integrated loudness from -16.2 to -15.8 LUFS and true peak at or below -1.0 dBTP. `g35.sh` builds the app and runs it with `G35_WAV` set. `f25.py` (J1.7) records a take through the control channel with a fake input; its run 4 has `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR` unset: `PREF set recordings_folder`, `RECORD quick`, restart, `RECORD quick`, and both takes land in the preference's folder. It also checks that a new take clears the Levels clip lights and that the meter shows the take, over `METERS` (J4.4). `f10.py` (I4.1) kills run 1 with `Popen.kill()` (Linux and Windows), accepts any non-zero exit code, and compares the samples after RECOVER with an `EXPORT` written before the kill (23 checks). `f04.py` also checks that each of two open files keeps its own zoom across a switch, with the View sync options off (A5.1), and `f66.py` checks the `SELBOX` verb on a 48 kHz file (B5.1). `b1-frames.py` (B1.4) makes a 3-hour 48 kHz stereo 16-bit WAV in a scratch folder, opens it with `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1`, zooms to 2 seconds with NAV, resets FRAMETIMES, scrolls with NAV for 10 seconds, and reads FRAMETIMES: it passes with count at least 300 and p95_ms at most 16.7. `--seconds N` makes a shorter file; `--selftest` runs its parsing and judging functions without the app. Default port 47201. `i2-restart.py` (I2.3) starts the app twice on one scratch home. Run 1 hides History with `PANEL close history`, drags the left divider 40 points with `DIVIDER drag` and quits with `QUIT`. Run 2 checks that History is still hidden and the left size is kept, then `WORKSPACE reset` brings History back and the first left size; it also checks that the log holds no ERROR line (15 checks in all). | `fNN.py --exe <release wavcraft> [--port N] [--keep]`, `k1.py --exe <release wavcraft>`, `l1.py --exe <release wavcraft>`, `g4_1.py --exe <release wavcraft> --kit <folder> [--long]`, `g35.py --exe <release wavcraft> --wav <long kit WAV> [--keep]`, `b1-frames.py --exe <release wavcraft> [--port N] [--seconds N] [--keep]`, `b1-frames.py --selftest`, `i2-restart.py --exe <release wavcraft> [--port N]`, `bash tools/alpha/fNN.sh`, `fNN-check.sh`, `fix286.py`, `i935.py` | 29 of the Python scripts import `Control` from tools/golden.py; the others carry their own client: 91 of them, k1.py, l1.py, g35.py, b1-frames.py and i2-restart.py among them, define their own `Control` class, and 14 use plain socket helpers. (g4_1.py defines its own `Control` class too.) cargo; CARGO_TARGET_DIR for the .sh wrappers. 135 Python and 188 shell files: 125 plain fNN.py, 126 plain fNN.sh, the rest suffixed variants plus fix286 (three files), i935, k1, l1, g4_1, b1-frames and i2-restart (g4_1.py and g4_1.sh are the two added here). ## `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` by line range (14,254 lines)

One struct, `WaveApp`, holds the whole window. Almost everything is a method in one `impl WaveApp` block (lines 936 to 12509). Each range starts at its first item, or at the doc comment just above it, and runs to the line before the next range, so no two ranges overlap.

Since 11e49a5 the file is 1,070 lines longer. These functions are new: `window_title`, `poll_transcript`, `cancel_opening`, `snap_playhead_drag`, `carry_play_to`, `go_to_marker_span`, `move_playhead`, `toolbar_item_on`, `check_drop_notice`, `send_title`, `effects_menu_rows`, `pressed_commands_with_focus`, `float_space_commands`, `command_key_pass`, `drop_notice`, `open_effect_dialog`, `favorites_menu`, `bars_source`, `toggle_effect_preview`, `stop_effect_preview`, `save_noise_print`, `load_noise_print`, `noise_print_summary`, `run_fade`, `draw_fade_handles`, `frame_timer_begin`, `frame_timer_end`, `run_script`, `select_entire_file`, `apply_panel_box` and `Tool::selects_time`, and the struct `ScriptBackend` is new too. `take_quality`, `take_meta` and `parse_save_args` are gone from this file. They now live in `crates/automation/src/lib.rs`. The `OPEN`, `SELECT`, `ACTION`, `EFFECT` and `SAVE` arms of `control_request` now read their line with `wavcraft_automation::Request::parse`, and only `SAVE` reaches those three, through `SaveArgs::parse`.

| Lines | What the range does |
|---|---|
| 1-98 | Module comment, imports, `mod spectral` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app/spectral.rs`), `FREE_SPACE_TTL` (how long the free-space figure is kept). The time ruler, frequency ruler, amplitude ruler and navigator sizes are not constants here: the file reads them through `theme::metrics()`. |
| 99-537 | Small private types and helpers: `MarqueeDrag`, `silence_frames` (the 24-hour limit of Insert Silence), `PrefPage`, `PrefAction`, `ScrubState`, `Hold`, `MicCheck`, `MicDialog`, the background-work records (`Opening`, `Running`, `Inserting`, `BatchWrite`, `Integrating`, `Making`, `Appending`, `SaveAllRun`, `Saving`), `NewAudio`, `AppendTo`, `Appended`, `DocSlot` (one parked document), `is_unsaved`, `closes_after_save`, `Import`, `Meta`, `window_title` (the title with its unsaved star), `SpectrumCache`, `HudState`, and `enum Tool` with its `impl`. |
| 538-898 | `pub struct WaveApp`: every field of the window (document slots, editor, view, selection, player, recorder, panels, settings, keymap, dialogs, worker handles, the Transcribe and Episode Check runs, the Episode Map classes the navigator last painted, the title last sent, the dropped-step count, the control channel receiver). |
| 899-935 | `ClipEdit`, `Scrub`, and the include-metadata setting helpers (`starting_include_meta`, `keep_include_meta`). |
| 936-1203 | `impl WaveApp` opens. `WaveApp::new`: resets the preferences first when `--reset-preferences` is given, loads settings, session folder (flag, then environment, then setting), keymap and the active shortcut set, panels, workspaces, recent files, export and convert presets, hardware profiles, starts the control channel, opens the file from the command line. |
| 1204-1338 | Look and view settings: `apply_look` (theme, colours), crossfades, snap (`apply_snap`, `keep_snap`, and `toggle_snap_menu`, which works with no file open), `view_text` for `NAV`, `frame_timer_begin` and `frame_timer_end` (the clock behind `FRAMETIMES`, B1.3), amplitude scale, zoom slots, channel layout, `save_settings`. |
| 1339-1617 | Preferences plumbing: `pref_set` (the `PREF` verb and dialog write through it), presets, audio hardware profiles (`remember_hardware`, `activate_setup`), input meter sync and `meter_peaks`, the mic check (start, poll, dialog), `poll_transcript` (a finished Transcribe run becomes one "Transcribe" step), `input_strip`, `reset_preferences`, `open_preferences`. |
| 1618-2126 | `preferences_dialog`: every Preferences page. |
| 2127-2579 | Opening audio: `open`, `open_as`, mixdown on open, `finish_opening`, Insert File, Open Many, the prompt that asks before an open drops unsaved edits (`request_open`, `answer_open_prompt`, `open_prompt_dialog`), the queue (`pump_open_queue`), `cancel_opening` (the status bar's Cancel and `OPEN_CANCEL`), Open Append (`start_append`, `finish_appending`). |
| 2580-3005 | Several documents at once: `take_slot`/`put_slot`/`switch_doc`, view sync, New Audio File (and its dialog), Copy to New, Paste to New, Close All, Save All, `run_doc_action`. |
| 3006-3587 | Files panel and friends: `close_doc`, `file_rows`, metadata refresh, `select_channel`, reveal in file manager, recent files, import (raw or other), `activate_row`, `files_panel`, `set_columns`, the `RECENT` and `FILES` request handlers, document tabs, file pickers. |
| 3588-3749 | Video attach and drop, `playhead_secs`, session recovery (`recover_session`, `recover_take`, discard), session store usage and cleanup, session folder picker and setter. |
| 3750-4218 | Panels and docking: show or hide, save layout, floating windows, monitors report, workspaces (snapshot, apply, commands, menu), dock zones, dividers, panel menu, dock drag. |
| 4219-4404 | Time format, snapping of a frame (`snap_at`, `snap_playhead_drag` for a playhead dragged along the ruler, `snap_gesture`), tempo, sample and hover readout, `op_state` and `window_state` for `STATE`, `fmt_time`, panel menu items, metadata display fields and dialog. |
| 4405-4678 | Recording glue: monitor attach, offset, `start_recording*`, timed start, `begin_recording`, `edit_lock`, record into the document (punch), `integrate_take`, `finish_integration`, `finish_recording`, `quick_record`. |
| 4679-4806 | `config_dir`, the noise print files (`noise_print_summary`, `save_noise_print` and `load_noise_print`, behind Effects › Save Noise Print… and Load Noise Print… and the `NOISE_PRINT` verb, G1.2), settings export, import and reload, the reset-preferences prompt, the About window. |
| 4807-5237 | Playback: `toggle_play`, `set_audio`, `carry_play_to` (a marker jump keeps the play going), `go_to_marker_span` (a Markers double-click or `MARKER select`), `halt_play`, follow mode, `start_play`, JKL shuttle, nudge, playhead step, `move_playhead` (Move Playhead to Previous / Next), scrub (`run_scrub`, `scrub_to`, `scrub_end`), pause, resume, stop, `poll_transport`, `transport_panel`. |

| 6745-7254 | Effects: the effect dialog, preview start and end (`toggle_effect_preview`, `stop_effect_preview`: the `EFFECT_DIALOG PREVIEW` verb, E6.2; `effect_dialog_ui` sends a changed setting to the preview worker through `set_params`, E6.3, and answers `Answer::SelectEntireFile` with `select_entire_file`, G2.6), generators and the Generate menu, `finish_effect_dialog`, `run_effect`, `run_fade` (a fade handle drag, E1.2), the Effects Rack (`apply_rack`, `rack_ui`, `sync_rack`), DeClip, DeClick, Diagnostics scan and repair, Markers, Playlist and Properties panels, the clear-history prompt. |
| 7255-7389 | `report` (error line), editing a multitrack clip's source in the waveform editor and returning to the session, `rebuild` (spectrogram and overview rebuild). |
| 7390-7629 | Background jobs: `start_job`, Convert Sample Type, Interpret Sample Rate, their dialogs, Match Loudness finish and panel, `start_test_job`, `finish_job`, `finish_rebuild`. |
| 7630-7934 | Saving: `unsaved`, `send_title` (sends the window title only when it changed), `any_unsaved`, `save`, `save_with` (format, sample type, metadata, worker thread; the Save As filter list comes from `v1::save_formats_in`), `save_copy`, `set_include_meta`, `finish_saving`. |
| 7935-8261 | Export window (formats, size estimate, presets, and the Dithering, Dither Type and Noise Shaping controls for a save below the file's depth, A4.4), the `SAVEOPTS` and `EXPORTPRESET` verbs, the close prompt (save, discard, cancel). |
| 8262-8474 | Selection keys, pointer gestures as data (`gesture`, the `GESTURE` verb), `playback_request` (the `PLAYBACK` verb), metronome and its verb. |
| 8475-10294 | `control_request`: the control channel's verb table, one match arm per verb (147 distinct first words, among them `SELBOX`, which types a time into a Selection box through `apply_panel_box` (B5); `TRANSCRIPT`, `CHECK`, `RUN_SCRIPT` and `EPISODEMAP` come first, then `STATE`). `MICCHECK` and `VIDEO frame` are answered earlier, in `logic`. |
| 10295-10380 | Playlist playback and the `PLAYLIST` and `MARKER` verbs. |
| 10381-10814 | Edit menu (with the Snapping submenu), shortcut changes and shortcut sets (requests and dialog), `effects_menu_rows` (the Effects menu's rows for a build; `v1.rs` tests the same fn), Effects menu, Recent menu, language switch, `action_text`. |
| 10815-11229 | `menu`: the whole menu bar, with the v1 gates on multitrack, CD layout, batch and video entries. The top-level menus are File, Edit, Multitrack, Window, Effects, Generate, View, Video and Help. |
| 11230-11444 | Status bar: free disk space, `status` (fields; the progress of an open, a job, Transcribe or Episode Check, each with Cancel; a stale Episode Check report; the error line). |
| 11445-11744 | Editor chrome: right click, spectral band (`set_band`), pointer gesture pick, tools bar, the heads-up display, `editor_shows`, zoom panel, zoom navigator (it paints the Episode Map columns in their class colours and keeps them for `EPISODEMAP`). |
| 11745-12510 | `editor`: the central view. Time ruler (a dragged playhead snaps), amplitude ruler, waveform lanes, spectral display with frequency ruler and divider, markers, selection and edge drags, fade handles, marquee and lasso, scrubbing, wheel zoom. The `impl WaveApp` block closes at 12509. |
| 12511-12524 | `panel_menu_button` helper (a free function, after the `impl` block). |
| 12525-12675 | `impl eframe::App`: `logic` runs every frame, shown or not. It collects worker results, puts the history drop notice up, ticks recording, transport, meters, the mic check, Transcribe, recovery, batch, playlist, and answers control channel requests. `on_exit` saves the window state. `logic` starts with `frame_timer_begin` (B1.3). |
| 12676-13007 | `ui`: per-frame drawing. The Episode Check poll, the record dialog, the close and open prompts, keyboard handling (history arrows, panel keys, record key, the keymap's commands, JKL), Shift-held reset, drag and drop, the window title, then menu, status, panels, editor, floating windows and every dialog, and it ends with `frame_timer_end` (B1.3). |
| 13008-13618 | Free helpers: reset of preference files, Import Raw parsing (`RawDialog`, `parse_import_raw`), `history_arrow_target`, dropped and split paths, `consume_record_now`, `tool_labels`, the key pass (`pressed_commands`, `pressed_commands_with_focus`, `float_space_commands`, `command_key_pass`: Alt+1 to Alt+4 and Alt+6 to Alt+8 toggle channels, Alt+5 is left to the key map), preference widgets, `snap_items` (the six Snapping items Edit and View share), ruler, fade handle (`draw_fade_handles`, E1.2) and marker drawing, colours and palette, preview seed, the two Help shortcut-list functions (`shortcut_help_rows`, `shortcut_help_content`, real since I3.1 but called only by tests until I3.2), export labels, test-only save hooks, `drop_notice`, `marker_drag_may_start`, `one_shot_effect`, `bars_source` (J4.3). |
| 13619-13986 | Tests, in-file: the `tests` module, 29 tests (the G1 noise print round trip over the control line, the Snap button's pressed state, one-shot effect names, channel chips, the marker drag rule, history arrows, key handling with and without a focused text field, tick labels, palettes, the Insert Silence day limit, `split_seed`, the include-metadata choice and the Lasso sharing the Select tool's time selection). |
| 13987-14003 | `ScriptBackend` (N4.5), not test code: it runs a script's lines on the window through `control_request` and maps the `OK ...` and `ERR ...` answers to the `Backend` result. `run_script` (in the History range above) uses it. It sits between the `tests` module and the test modules that follow. |
| 14004-14254 | Tests, pulled in and small: one in-file test each in `reset_tests`, `step_name_tests` and `look_tests`, and 38 test files pulled in with `#[path]` (161 more tests, each `shortcut_case!` case counted once) from `apps/wavcraft/src/`: `a1_open_cancel_tests.rs` (3), `a2_close_prompt_tests.rs` (2), `a2_title_star_tests.rs` (2), `a3_mp3_tests.rs` (6), `b1_frametimes_tests.rs` (5), `b2_1_spectral_toggle_tests.rs` (2), `b2_3_freq_scale_tests.rs` (1), `b2_5_edit_picture_tests.rs` (5), `c3_playhead_snap_tests.rs` (5), `c5_6_space_button_tests.rs` (2), `c5_playhead_move_tests.rs` (2), `c5_space_return_tests.rs` (2), `d2_new_from_clip_tests.rs` (1), `d5_drop_notice_tests.rs` (2), `e5_repeat_dialog_tests.rs` (2), `e6_effect_preview_tests.rs` (6), `f3_2_effect_preset_tests.rs` (4), `g2_4_noise_dialog_tests.rs` (2), `g3_alt5_tests.rs` (2), `h1_3_marker_selection_tests.rs` (1), `h1_star_marker_tests.rs` (1), `h2_7_double_click_tests.rs` (1), `h3_marker_jump_tests.rs` (3), `i3_shortcut_help_tests.rs` (41), `issue1000_tests.rs` (7), `issue1002_tests.rs` (7), `issue1173_tests.rs` (2), `issue1219_tests.rs` (6), `issue366_tests.rs` (12), `issue369_tests.rs` (7), `issue377_tests.rs` (2), `j1_recordings_row_tests.rs` (2), `j4_meter_tests.rs` (1), `j6_9_state_tests.rs` (1), `m2_map_tests.rs` (1), `n1_parity_tests.rs` (4), `n3_mcp_window_tests.rs` (1), `n4_run_script_tests.rs` (5). The other `*_tests.rs` files there, `f48_device_change_tests.rs` and `j2_no_output_tests.rs`, are pulled in by `player.rs`. |


## Tests

One row per test file. A test that is ignored is counted in "Tests"; a stub's tests are ignored until the stub is filled. A test made by a macro (`shortcut_case!`, `shortcuts!`) counts once per case.

### App tests (`apps/wavcraft/src/*_tests.rs`)

| `crates/core/tests/save_mp3.rs` | Locks the MP3 save. A3.3: `from_path("a.MP3")` and `parse(" mp3 ")` give Mp3, its name is MP3 and its extension mp3, and it accepts float samples (`mp3_is_a_save_format`). A3.4: a stereo tone saved with `save_mp3` at 192 kbps and with `save` reopens with the same channels, rate and length and the same level, and the file has the finished Info tag (`a_stereo_tone_round_trips_at_192`); a failed save leaves the old target whole and no temporary file (`a_failed_mp3_save_leaves_the_target_whole`); a 96000 Hz file is refused before any file is made (`a_96k_file_is_refused`). A3.5 (#1464): with `mono` set a stereo document saves as one channel, the average of the two (`mono_from_stereo_is_the_average`), and a mono document is unchanged (`mono_on_a_mono_document_is_unchanged`). A3.10: the constant-bit-rate estimate is 240000 bytes for 10 s 44.1 kHz stereo at 192 kbps and about 80000 for 5 s 48 kHz mono at 128 kbps, not exact (`the_mp3_estimate_is_the_cbr_size`), and the estimate refuses a 96000 Hz document and a 100 kbps choice as a save would (`the_mp3_estimate_refuses_what_the_save_would`). | 8 tests, 0 ignored | A3.3, A3.4, A3.5, A3.10, #1312 | save::SaveFormat, save, save_mp3, estimate_size, estimate_mp3_size, mp3_options::Mp3Options |

| `apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs` | Locks `wavcraft convert` (N2.2), `wavcraft inspect` (N2.3) and `wavcraft run` (N2.4) by running the built binary (`CARGO_BIN_EXE_wavcraft`) with DISPLAY and WAYLAND_DISPLAY removed and `--session-dir` set to a scratch folder: a 16-bit WAV to FLAC and back is sample-identical and leaves no session (`wav_to_flac_and_back_is_identical`); a bad flag (`a_bad_flag_exits_2_with_a_message`) and an unknown output extension, `no save format` (`an_unknown_extension_is_refused`), exit 2 with a message; a good and a failed run leave no session (`no_session_is_left_behind`); a missing input and an output in a folder that does not exist exit 2 with a message and leave no session (`a_missing_input_exits_2_with_a_message_and_leaves_no_session`, `an_unwritable_output_exits_2_with_a_message_and_leaves_no_session`); `inspect` of a 1 s, 48 kHz, stereo WAV prints the Properties rows and exits 0 with empty standard error and no session left (`inspect_prints_the_properties_rows`); a missing file exits 2 with its name on standard error (`inspect_of_a_missing_file_exits_2_with_its_name`); `run` applies a two-step script and saves (`run_applies_a_two_step_script`), gives the same file as `Headless::run_script` (`run_matches_headless_run_script`), and on a bad line writes no output file (`a_bad_line_writes_nothing`). | 15 tests, 0 ignored. The four `mcp` tests (N3.5, #1381) run `wavcraft mcp` and `wavcraft mcp --attach` with standard input piped and the window's and control port's variables removed: `mcp_answers_tools_list_on_stdio`, `mcp_runs_a_call_on_stdio`, `mcp_attach_without_a_port_exits_2`, `mcp_refuses_a_bad_flag` | N2.2, #1299; N2.3, #1300; N2.4, #1504; N3.5, #1381 | apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs (`convert`, `run_script_file`, `serve_mcp`), cli.rs, wavcraft_automation::mcp, wavcraft_automation::headless, wavcraft_core::Document |

## WavCraft control verbs (main at 92d0f13)

How a line travels: `apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs` listens on loopback (only when `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` and `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE` are set), checks the `TOKEN <token>` first line, and hands each later line, trimmed, to the window thread. There the request loop in `logic` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs`, from line 12081) takes `MICCHECK` and `VIDEO frame` first. A good one is answered later, when the listening time is up or when the video worker has the picture. A bad one gets its `ERR` at once. Every other line goes to `WaveApp::control_request` (lines 8107 to 9852), which matches the first word: 144 distinct first words, `TRANSCRIPT` first and `QUIT` last, then `ERR unknown request "<word>"` for anything else. With `MICCHECK` and `TOKEN`, the tables below list 146. Some arms answer in place, some pass the rest of the line to another file. A `DIAG scan` that starts also answers late: `logic` holds its reply until the scan ends. `control.rs` itself handles only `TOKEN`.

Verbs marked "not in header" have a match arm but no line in the `control.rs` header comment.

`crates/automation/src/lib.rs` has the shared parser, `Request::parse`, for six of these (`OPEN`, `SELECT`, `ACTION`, `EFFECT`, `SAVE`, `STATE`). The window uses it for `OPEN`, `SELECT`, `ACTION <name>`, `EFFECT <name>` and `SAVE`, so a bad line gets the same `ERR` text with or without the window. A bare `EFFECT` (the list), `ACTION window.*` and `STATE` keep the window's own arms. The window's `STATE` ignores anything after the word, which the shared parser refuses. The headless path runs the six with no window (`headless.rs`; `wavcraft convert <in> <out>` in `apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs` is one `OPEN` and one `SAVE` through it) or sends them to a running window (`remote.rs`, which waits after `OPEN` and `SAVE` for `busy=0` and then reads `LASTERROR`; only its tests use it so far). Its `SAVE` takes the same trailing options as the window's (`bits=`, `float=`, `aiff=`, `quality=`, `meta=`) and a bare `SAVE` saves in place. `apps/wavcraft/src/n1_parity_tests.rs` runs the same steps through `control_request` and through `Headless` and compares the saved files sample for sample. `crates/automation/src/mcp.rs` (`mcp::handle`) answers MCP's `initialize`, `tools/list` (seven tools: `open`, `select`, `action`, `effect`, `state`, `save`, `list_effects`) and `tools/call` (N3.2: the six request tools run on the Backend, `list_effects` gives `effects::describe`). Only `crates/automation/tests/mcp.rs` and `crates/automation/tests/mcp_tasks.rs` call `handle`. The comment at the top of `lib.rs` still says the window keeps its own copy of the `SAVE` option parser. It has not kept one since fb26fb9.

### Connection

| Verb | Meaning | Handled in |
|---|---|---|
| `TOKEN <token>` | First line of every connection; must match the owner-only token file | control.rs (`serve`) |

| `RUN_SCRIPT <path>` | Run the script file at path on the open document as one undo step 'Run Script'; answers `OK replaced=<n>`, or `ERR line N: reason` after putting everything back. A missing file, no path, no open file, a save or job running, or a script over 1 MiB is refused | app.rs (`run_script`, `ScriptBackend`) |
| `EPISODEMAP` | The Episode Map as the zoom navigator last painted it: `OK cols=<n> silence=<n> speech=<n> loud=<n>`, the strip's columns and how many are each class (not in header) | app.rs, classes from `wavcraft_core::episode_map` |

The list keeps the newest 20000 frames | app.rs (`frame_timer_begin`, `frame_timer_end`), `frame_stats::summarize_frames` |

| `OPEN_CANCEL` | Cancel the file that is opening, as the status bar's Cancel does: no document is added and the files queued behind it are dropped. `ERR no file is opening` when none is | app.rs (`cancel_opening`) |
| `OPEN_MANY <paths>` | Open several files, each as its own document. Paths are split on spaces and tabs. Answers `OK queued=<n>` (not in header) | app.rs |

| `IMPORT <path>` | Add a file to the Files panel without opening it (not in header) | app.rs |

| `SWITCH <i>` | Make document `i` the current one | app.rs |
| `CLOSE [discard]` | Close the current document | app.rs |
| `SAVE [path] [bits= float= aiff= quality= meta= bitrate= channels=]` | Save, or Save As with the format from the extension (`.wav`, `.aiff` or `.aif`, `.flac`, `.ogg` or `.oga`, `.w64`, `.caf`, `.au` or `.snd`; a name with no extension gets `.wav` and is saved as a WAV). A `.mp3` path is written as MP3 through `save_mp3` (A3.6): 192 kbps stereo unless `bitrate=<kbps>` (one of 32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 80, 96, 112, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 320) or `channels=<1\|2>` is given, and a bitrate or channel choice stays for the later saves of the window. The headless `SAVE` and `wavcraft convert` write MP3 the same way (A3.11); the headless `SAVE` keeps the choice for later saves too | app.rs (`save_with`), parsed by `wavcraft_automation::Request::parse` |
| `SAVE_CANCEL` | Stop the running save | app.rs |
| `SAVE_SELECTION <path>` | Save only the selection to a new WAV | app.rs |
| `EXPORT <path>` | Export the whole document to another WAV | app.rs |
| `EXPORT_RANGES <folder> [pattern=]` | Each range marker as its own WAV | app.rs |

| `EXPORTPRESET save, load, delete, list` | Named export option presets | app.rs (`control_export_preset`) |
