## Command-line flags and environment variables (main at 92d0f13)

I found these by grepping the workspace for `env::var`, `var_os`, `vars_os`, `env!`, `.env(`, `args_os`, `args()` and `--` literals, and the scripts in `tools/` for `os.environ`, `add_argument` and `$NAME`. Every row was checked against the file at `92d0f13`.

### The app binary: `wavcraft` (`apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs`, `apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs`)

`main` handles the command line in this order:

1. `--vst3-scan BUNDLE` as the first argument: the scan worker (below). Nothing else is read.
2. `cli::parse` turns the rest into a `cli::Command`. It is a pure parser: it opens no file and prints nothing. On an error, `exit_error` prints the message on standard error and exits 2.
3. `Command::Convert` and `Command::Inspect` run and exit, before the log and the window start.
4. `Command::Mcp` (N3.5) runs and exits, before the log and the window start: `serve_mcp` answers each standard-input line through `mcp::handle` on `Headless`, or on `Remote::from_env` for `mcp --attach`, and exits 0 at the end of input.
5. `Command::Run` (N2.4) runs and exits, before the log and the window start: `run_script_file` opens the file through `Headless`, runs the script text with `Headless::run_script`, saves to `-o` only when every line passed, and exits 0, or 2 with the reason (a bad line reads `line N: reason`).
6. `Command::Window` installs the log (`selflog::install`), sets up graphics (`configure_graphics`) and opens the window.

A subcommand (`convert`, `inspect`, `run` or `mcp`) is only seen as the first argument. The one exception is `convert`, `inspect` or `run` right after `--session-dir DIR` or `--session-dir=DIR`. Any other line is a window line. So `wavcraft --reset-preferences convert a.wav b.flac` opens the window, on a file named `convert`, and `wavcraft --session-dir D run s.txt` is a `run` line.

| Flag | What it does | Read in |
|---|---|---|
| `FILE` | The first argument that is not a flag, and not the value after `--session-dir`, is opened in the window. Later bare arguments are ignored. A lone `-` counts as a file name. | `main.rs` `parse_args`, called from `cli.rs` `window` |
| `--session-dir DIR` or `--session-dir=DIR` | Session store folder for this run. Wins over `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR` and File › Session Folder…. An empty value is skipped. A folder that fails `settings::check_folder` gives the default (`wavcraft/sessions` in the cache folder) and a warning. A `--session-dir` with no value after it is ignored. | `main.rs` `parse_args`; `app.rs` `WaveApp::new`; `crates/core/src/settings.rs` `resolve_session_folder` |
| `--reset-preferences` | Before the settings are read, moves `settings.txt` aside as `settings.txt.reset-N` and starts with the defaults. `shortcuts.txt` and the workspaces are kept. The same reset as the "Reset all preferences" button in Preferences. | `main.rs` `parse_args`; `app.rs` `reset_preference_files`; `crates/core/src/bundle.rs` `reset_preferences` |
| any other `-x` or `--name` | An error since #1298: `unknown option '<flag>'` and the window's usage line, exit 2. Before that, an unknown flag was taken as the file name. There is no `--help` or `--version`: both are unknown options. | `cli.rs` `window`, `is_flag` |

| Command | What it does | Read in |
|---|---|---|
| `wavcraft convert <in> <out>` | Present, wired (N2.2). Opens `<in>` and saves `<out>` with no window: `Headless::new(sessions)`, then `Request::Open` and `Request::Save` with `SaveArgs::default()`. No log file and no crash hook, since `selflog::install` comes later. Exit 0, or exit 2 with the reason on standard error. Details below. | `cli.rs` `convert`; `main.rs` `convert`, `conversion_sessions_dir`; `crates/automation/src/headless.rs` |
| `wavcraft run <script> [FILE] [-o OUT]` | Present, wired (N2.4). Opens FILE through `Headless`, runs the script text with `Headless::run_script`, and only when every line passed saves to OUT (with no `-o`, nothing is saved); exit 0, or 2 with the reason on standard error (a bad line reads `line N: reason`); before the log or a window starts. With no FILE it exits 2 with the usage line. `-o` may come anywhere after `run`. It takes `--session-dir DIR` or `--session-dir=DIR` like `convert` and uses it for its session store. | `cli.rs` `run`, `scan`; `main.rs` `run_script_file`, `main`; `crates/automation/src/headless.rs` `run_script` |
| `wavcraft inspect <file>` | Present, wired (N2.3). Opens `<file>` with `Document::open_in` into the session folder (`--session-dir`, else `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR`, else `wavcraft-sessions` in the temp folder), then writes each row of `Document::properties` on standard output as `Key: value`, one per line, and exits 0. Like `convert`, it does not read File › Session Folder…. A file that cannot be opened is `<file>: <reason>` on standard error, exit 2. The document is dropped before the process exits, so no session folder is left. | `cli.rs` `inspect`; `main.rs` `inspect`, `main` |
| `wavcraft mcp [--attach]` | Parsed into `Command::Mcp` (N3.5). Reads standard input one line at a time, answers each through `crates/automation/src/mcp.rs` `handle` (`initialize`, `tools/list`, `tools/call`) and writes each reply as one line on standard output. Plain `mcp` uses `Headless` with the session folder from `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR` (else the temp folder); `--attach` uses `Remote::from_env` and exits 2 naming `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` when it is not set. Any other flag or operand exits 2 with the usage line. Standard error carries the messages. No window and no log. | `cli.rs` `parse`, `mcp`; `main.rs` `serve_mcp`, `serve` |
| `wavcraft --vst3-scan BUNDLE` | Hidden worker mode. Must be the first argument. Prints one VST3 bundle's report on standard output (one line per effect, starting with `WAVCRAFT-VST3`; any other line, such as a plugin's own output, is ignored by `parse_worker_output`) and exits 0, with no window and no log. With no `BUNDLE` it prints nothing and exits 0. The app starts itself this way to scan plugins (`vst3::scan_with`, from `rack_panel.rs`). Anywhere else on the line, `--vst3-scan` is an unknown option. | `main.rs` `main`; `WORKER_FLAG` and `WORKER_TAG` in `crates/core/src/vst3.rs` |

Not in the binary: the batch parser in `crates/core/src/batch_cli.rs` (`--batch`, `--out`, `--name`, `--bits`, `--step`, `--`) is present, core only. `crates/core/tests/batch_cli.rs` tests it, but nothing in the app calls it. `Remote::from_env` in `crates/automation/src/remote.rs` reads `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` and `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE`; its callers are the example `crates/automation/examples/headless_window_parity.rs` (N1.7) and `serve_mcp` in `main.rs` for `mcp --attach` (N3.5).

### `convert` in detail

- **Session folder.** `--session-dir`, else `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR`, else `wavcraft-sessions` in the temp folder. Unlike the window, `convert` does not read File › Session Folder…, does not skip an empty value and does not check the folder. The `Headless` backend is dropped when `main.rs` `convert` returns, on success and on failure, so no session is left in that folder.
- **Where `--session-dir` may go.** `cli::parse` takes the line as a `convert`, `inspect` or `run` line when that word is the first argument, or comes right after `--session-dir DIR` or `--session-dir=DIR`. It then takes every `--session-dir` out of the line wherever it is, so `convert a.wav b.flac --session-dir D` works too. The last one wins. A `--session-dir` with no value is the error `--session-dir needs a directory`, with no usage line. `run` uses it for its session store like `convert` (N2.4).
- **Output format.** The extension of `<out>` picks it, as in `Headless::save`: `wav`, `aif` or `aiff`, `flac`, `ogg` or `oga`, `w64`, `caf`, `au` or `snd`. A name with no extension is saved as a WAV, with `.wav` added. `mp3` is written by `save_mp3` at 192 kbps stereo, since `convert` passes no `bitrate=` or `channels=` (A3.11). Any other extension, `wave` and `bwf` included, fails with `no save format for <path>`.
- **What the save keeps.** The source's sample type, or the nearest one the format holds. For a WAV, "Include markers and other metadata" is on (`Session::include_meta` starts true). Ogg Vorbis uses `VORBIS_DEFAULT_QUALITY`. The window's settings file is not read.

### Messages and exit codes

The usage lines are the constants `WINDOW_USAGE`, `RUN_USAGE`, `CONVERT_USAGE`, `INSPECT_USAGE` and `MCP_USAGE` in `cli.rs`:

```
usage: wavcraft [--session-dir DIR] [--reset-preferences] [FILE]
usage: wavcraft run <script> [FILE] [-o OUT]
usage: wavcraft convert <in> <out>
usage: wavcraft inspect <file>
usage: wavcraft mcp [--attach]
```

| Case | Standard error | Exit |
|---|---|---|
| Unknown flag, no subcommand | `unknown option '<flag>'`, then the window's usage line | 2 |
| Unknown flag after `run`, `convert`, `inspect` or `mcp` | `unknown option '<flag>'`, then that subcommand's usage line | 2 |
| `mcp` with an operand | `unexpected argument '<operand>'`, then `usage: wavcraft mcp [--attach]` | 2 |
| `mcp --attach` with no `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` | `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT is not set`; nothing is read from standard input | 2 |
| `mcp` (or `mcp --attach`) at the end of standard input | nothing; each reply is one line on standard output | 0 |
| Too few operands | `missing operand`, then the usage line | 2 |
| Too many operands | `unexpected argument '<first extra one>'`, then the usage line | 2 |
| `run` with `-o` last | `-o needs a file name`, then `run`'s usage line | 2 |
| `convert` with `--session-dir` and no value | `--session-dir needs a directory` | 2 |
| `convert` cannot open or save | the error text, for example `no save format for <path>` | 2 |
| `run` with no FILE | `usage: wavcraft run <script> <file> [-o <out>]` | 2 |
| `run`, the script cannot be read | `<script>: <reason>` | 2 |
| `run`, a script line is refused | `line N: reason`, and no output file is written | 2 |
| `run` succeeds | nothing on standard output; `<out>` is saved when `-o` was given | 0 |
| `inspect`, the file cannot be opened | `<file>: cannot read the file: ...`, or the decoding, empty or cancelled reason | 2 |
| `inspect` succeeds | the Properties rows on standard output, `Key: value` each | 0 |
| `convert` succeeds | nothing | 0 |
| `--vst3-scan` | nothing (the report is on standard output) | 0 |
| The window opens and is closed | nothing, unless the log folder cannot be made (`wavcraft: no log file in <dir>: <error>`) | 0 |
| The window cannot start | `Error: ...`, printed by Rust because `main` returns eframe's error | 1 |

On Windows a release build is a windowed program (`windows_subsystem = "windows"` in `main.rs`), so started from a terminal it shows none of these messages: they can only be read by redirecting standard error to a file or a pipe. N2.5 is the step that fixes this.

Tests: `apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs` runs the built binary (`CARGO_BIN_EXE_wavcraft`) with `DISPLAY` and `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` removed and `--session-dir <scratch>` added last (the `mcp` tests set `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR` and pipe standard input instead). It has 15 tests. Six cover `convert` and a bad flag: `wav_to_flac_and_back_is_identical`, `a_bad_flag_exits_2_with_a_message`, `an_unknown_extension_is_refused`, `no_session_is_left_behind`, `a_missing_input_exits_2_with_a_message_and_leaves_no_session` and `an_unwritable_output_exits_2_with_a_message_and_leaves_no_session`. Two cover `inspect` (`inspect_prints_the_properties_rows`, `inspect_of_a_missing_file_exits_2_with_its_name`), four cover `mcp` (`mcp_answers_tools_list_on_stdio`, `mcp_runs_a_call_on_stdio`, `mcp_attach_without_a_port_exits_2`, `mcp_refuses_a_bad_flag`) and three cover `run` (N2.4: `run_applies_a_two_step_script`, `run_matches_headless_run_script`, `a_bad_line_writes_nothing`). `cli.rs` has 13 unit tests for the parser (the seven before N2.3, four for `inspect` and two for `mcp`), while `main.rs` has 1 (`the_session_dir_flag_and_the_file_in_any_order`). No test covers `--session-dir DIR convert` in the form with a space, or `--vst3-scan` (only the app's own scans run it; `tools/alpha/f128.py` drives one).

### Environment variables the app reads

Nothing in the workspace calls `std::env::set_var`. A test that needs a variable starts a child process with it.

| Variable | Value | Effect | Read in |
|---|---|---|---|
| `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR` | folder | Window: session store folder, over File › Session Folder… and under `--session-dir`. An empty value is skipped and the folder is checked. `convert`: taken as it is, over the temp-folder default. | `app.rs` `WaveApp::new` (`settings::ENV_SESSION_DIR` in `crates/core/src/settings.rs`); `main.rs` `conversion_sessions_dir` (the name written out, not the constant) |
| `WAVCRAFT_LOG_DIR` | folder | Where the log (`wavcraft.log`, with the two runs before kept beside it) and crash files go. Default is `wavcraft/logs` in the config folder, or in the temp folder when there is no config folder. An empty value is ignored. If the folder cannot be made, the app prints `wavcraft: no log file in <dir>: <error>` and runs without a log. `convert` and `--vst3-scan` write no log. | `selflog.rs` `log_dir`, `install` |
| `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` | port number | Turns the control channel on at 127.0.0.1 on that port. Needs `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE` too. A value that is not a port number leaves the channel off, with nothing logged. | `control.rs` `start_from_env`; `crates/automation/src/remote.rs` `Remote::from_env` (called by the example `crates/automation/examples/headless_window_parity.rs`, N1.7, and by `serve_mcp` in `apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs` for `mcp --attach`, N3.5) |
| `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_TOKEN_FILE` | file path | The control channel's token. A missing file is made with a new token of 64 hex digits, for the owner only. An existing file must be a plain file (not a link), closed to other users, and hold at least 64 hex digits. Otherwise the channel stays off, with a warning in the log. | `control.rs` `token`, `check_private` (on Windows through `win_acl.rs`); `remote.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR` | folder | Where takes are written, and where Recovery looks for unfinished ones. Default is `WavCraft Recordings` in the audio folder, else the documents folder, else the home folder. An empty value is ignored. The `recordings_folder` setting (`crates/core/src/settings.rs`, #1305) is stored, but nothing reads it yet. | `recording.rs` `record_dir` |
| `WAVCRAFT_PEAK_CACHE_DIR` | folder | Where saved waveform overviews live. Default is `wavcraft/peaks` in the system cache folder, or in the temp folder. An empty value is not skipped. | `app.rs` `WaveApp::new`; default in `crates/core/src/peakcache.rs` `default_dir` |
| `WAVCRAFT_VST3_PATH` | path list (the OS's own separator) | Folders to scan for VST3 plugins, in place of the standard ones (`vst3::default_dirs`). For tests. | `rack_panel.rs` `vst3_dirs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_MONITORS` | `name:x,y,w,h;name:...` | Fakes the monitor list (first is the primary). A value that does not parse is ignored, with a warning in the log. The `MONITORS simulate` verb takes the same text. | `app.rs` `WaveApp::new`; parser `crates/core/src/monitors.rs` `parse` |
| `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO` | any value but empty, `0`, `false` | Swaps the sound card for a silent real-time test sink, named `test sink (no audio)`. The multitrack transport only accepts exactly `1`. | `player.rs` `no_audio`; `multitrack_ui.rs` `Transport::no_audio` |
| `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO_RATE` | rate in Hz, 8000 to 768000 | Rate of the silent sink. Default 48000, also for a value out of range. | `player.rs` `Sink::start` |
| `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_INPUT` | `sine:<hz>[+<hz>...]` (up to 32 tones, one per input), with optional `,ring=<ms>` and `,stall=<ms>` | Replaces the input device with generated tones when recording. A bad value is an error when a recording starts. Any value unlocks the `HOLD_BUSY` control verb. The input monitor parses the same variable its own way: `sine:<hz>[:<dBFS>]` (level -12 by default) or `silence`, so a value with `+`, `ring=` or `stall=` is refused there. The multitrack recorder only accepts exactly `1`, which gives 8 fake inputs. | `crates/core/src/record.rs` (`ENV_FAKE_INPUT`, `FakeSpec::from_env`, called from `recording.rs`); `input_monitor.rs` `InputMonitor::new`; `app.rs` `control_request`; `crates/core/src/mtrecord.rs` `fake_input` |
| `WAVCRAFT_MONITOR_SINK` | `null` | While recording with monitoring, drains the monitor queue in a thread, not through an output device. | `recording.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH` | exactly `1` | Transcribe (`transcript.run`, and the `TRANSCRIPT run` control verb) uses the fake speech engine, `FakeEngine` in `crates/core/src/transcript/run.rs`: one word `w<k>` per window, whatever the audio. Without it, Transcribe fails with `no speech engine available; set WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH=1`. There is no real engine yet. | `transcript_run.rs` `TranscriptRun::start` |
| `WAVCRAFT_TEST_SAVE_CHUNK` | set | Test only. A save with "Include markers and other metadata" on adds a `TEST` chunk to the file. | `app.rs` `metadata_chunks` |
| `WAVCRAFT_TEST_SAVE_DELAY_MS` | milliseconds | Test only. Slows a save down per 64 K-frame block. | `app.rs` `test_block_delay` |
| `WGPU_BACKEND` | wgpu backend name | On Windows the app forces DirectX 12 unless this is set. When it is set, the app leaves the choice to eframe and wgpu. | `main.rs` `configure_graphics` |
| `CRAFT_LOG` | a level: `off`, `error`, `warn`, `info`, `debug` or `trace` | Log level for the app's own lines. `CRAFT_LOG=debug` gives the filter `warn,wavcraft=debug`, so other crates stay at warn. Wins over `RUST_LOG`. Any other value is skipped. The app's default is `warn,wavcraft=info` (`selflog.rs` `APP`). | `crates/craft-log/src/filter.rs` `spec`, `spec_from_env`, used by `selflog.rs` |
| `RUST_LOG` | filter text | Log filter, used as it is, when `CRAFT_LOG` is unset or not a level. An empty value is skipped. | `crates/craft-log/src/filter.rs` `spec`, `spec_from_env` |
| `CRAFT_LOG_JSON` | `1`, `true`, `yes` | One JSON object per log line, in craft-log's own command-line logger. The app's logger (`selflog.rs`) does not call it. | `crates/craft-log/src/filter.rs` `json_from_env`, called only from `crates/craft-log/src/cli.rs` |
| `CRAFT_LOG_STDERR` | `1`, `true`, `yes` | Log to standard error too, in craft-log's own command-line logger. The app's logger does not call it. | `crates/craft-log/src/cli.rs` |

Mismatches I saw in the code:

- `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO` and `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_INPUT` are each tested two ways: any truthy value or a tone spec in the waveform editor, exactly `1` in the multitrack code.
- `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_INPUT` has two tone parsers: `FakeSpec::parse` in `crates/core/src/record.rs` for recording, and `FakeInput::parse` in `crates/core/src/input_meter.rs` for the input monitor. `record.rs` also has a type named `FakeInput`: the tone generator for recording.
- `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR` is read two ways. The window skips an empty value and checks the folder. `convert` does neither, and writes the name out instead of using `settings::ENV_SESSION_DIR`.
- `WAVCRAFT_PEAK_CACHE_DIR` does not skip an empty value. `WAVCRAFT_LOG_DIR` and `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR` do.
- `run`'s parser usage has `[FILE]` as optional. The v1 list (`docs/v1/v1.md`, N2) and N2.4 have `run <script> <file>`; `main` answers a missing FILE with `usage: wavcraft run <script> <file> [-o <out>]` and exit 2.

craft-log's system report (`crates/craft-log/src/report.rs`) and its command-line logger (`cli.rs`) read more variables: `XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP`, `XDG_SESSION_TYPE`, `WAYLAND_DISPLAY`, `DISPLAY`, `GAMESCOPE_WAYLAND_DISPLAY`, `SteamGamepadUI`, `LC_ALL`, `LANG`, `PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER`, `HOME`, `USERPROFILE`, `HOMEDRIVE`, `HOMEPATH`, `PATH`, and a scan of the whole environment (`vars_os`) for switches (`CRAFT_*`, `RUST_LOG` and the app's own prefixes) and for secrets (names with `TOKEN`, `SECRET`, `PASSWORD` and the like). The app does not reach that code. It only calls `App::filter_spec` (which reads `CRAFT_LOG` and `RUST_LOG`), `install_wrapped`, `set_log_path` and `crash::install_hook` (`selflog.rs`), and uses craft-log's `Filter` and `Line` types, so of craft-log's variables only `CRAFT_LOG` and `RUST_LOG` act on the app.

The app's config, cache, audio and documents folders come from the `dirs` crate, not from a variable of mine. The check scripts that start the app (for example `tools/alpha/f43.py`, `f82.py`, `k1.py`, `l1.py`) and `tools/golden.py` give it a scratch home by setting `HOME`, and most also `USERPROFILE` (102 of the 132) and `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` (125). They all set `WAVCRAFT_SESSION_DIR`, and all but `k1.py` and `l1.py` set `WAVCRAFT_LOG_DIR`, since on Windows the config folder comes from the system, not from `APPDATA` (`selflog.rs` `log_dir`).

### Variables used only by tests, tools and the build

| Variable | Effect | Read in |
|---|---|---|
| `WAVCRAFT_KIT` | Folder holding the 3-hour test kit file. The default is a hard-coded Windows folder. | `crates/testkit/tests/edits.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_CLEAN_SPEECH` | Path of a clean speech WAV, not in the repo, for the ignored test `a_real_clean_speech_file_reports_nothing` (K2.5). | `crates/core/tests/episode_check.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_J3_PROCESS_KILL_CHILD`, `WAVCRAFT_J3_PROCESS_KILL_DIR` | The killed-process test runs itself again as a child with these set, and with `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_INPUT=sine:440`, then kills it (J3.3). | `crates/core/tests/j3_process_kill.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1` (set for a child) | Fifteen app test files run their test again in a child process with it set, so the child uses the silent sink. Seeing exactly `1` tells the test it is the child. | `b2_1_spectral_toggle_tests.rs`, `b2_3_freq_scale_tests.rs`, `c5_playhead_move_tests.rs`, `c5_space_return_tests.rs`, `d5_drop_notice_tests.rs`, `e5_repeat_dialog_tests.rs`, `e6_effect_preview_tests.rs`, `f48_device_change_tests.rs`, `g2_4_noise_dialog_tests.rs`, `h1_3_marker_selection_tests.rs`, `h2_7_double_click_tests.rs`, `h3_marker_jump_tests.rs`, `issue377_tests.rs`, `j2_no_output_tests.rs`, `m2_map_tests.rs` in `apps/wavcraft/src/` |
| `DISPLAY`, `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` (removed for a child) | Removed so the test shows `convert` needs no display. | `apps/wavcraft/tests/cli.rs` |
| `WAVCRAFT_SCRATCH` | Scratch folder for scripted checks. `f70.sh` defaults it to a folder under the home folder. | six `tools/alpha/*.py` scripts (`f03`, `f10`, `f16`, `f37`, `f70`, `f75`) and `tools/alpha/f70.sh` |
| `ALPHA_SCRATCH` | Scratch folder, else the temp folder. `f54.sh` sets it for `f54.py`. | `tools/alpha/` `f25.py`, `f34.py`, `f44.py`, `f54.py`, `f55.py`, `f77.py` |
| `F122_SCRATCH`, `F129_SCRATCH`, `F76_SCRATCH_BASE`, `F82_SCRATCH_ROOT` | Scratch folders for single scripts. | `tools/alpha/` `f122.py`, `f129.py`, `f76.py`, `f82.py` |
| `WAVCRAFT_SCRATCH_ROOT` | Same idea, older name. | `tools/alpha/f19.py`, `f68.py`, `f69.py` |
| `WAVCRAFT_CONTROL_PORT` | Replaces the script's default port (47143, 47148) for the app it starts. | `tools/alpha/f43.py`, `f48.py` |
| `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` | Where the scripts look for the release binary, `$CARGO_TARGET_DIR/release/wavcraft` (`f05-bench.sh` also takes `wavcraft-bench` from there, and `f79-all.sh` writes `clippy.txt` there). There is no default: 130 of the 186 `tools/alpha/*.sh` scripts need it set. `tools/macos/bundle.sh` defaults to `target`. | `tools/alpha/*.sh`, `tools/macos/bundle.sh` |
| `KEEP`, `F97_SAMPLE`, `F82_PORT`, `F84_ARGS` | Per-script switches: `KEEP=1` keeps `f64.py`'s scratch folder after a failure; `F97_SAMPLE` runs macOS `sample` on the app when `f97.py` fails; `F82_PORT` (default 47182) is `f82.sh`'s port; `F84_ARGS` are extra arguments for `f84.py`. | `tools/alpha/f64.py`, `f97.py`, `f82.sh`, `f84.sh` |
| `SOUNDCRAFT_DIR` | Where the sibling engine checkout is. The default is a folder under the home folder. | `tools/alpha/f128.sh` |
| `CRAFT_LOG_COMMIT`, `CRAFT_LOG_TREE`, `CRAFT_LOG_BUILD_DATE` | Compile-time values set by the build script. | `crates/craft-log/build.rs`, `crates/craft-log/src/lib.rs` |
| `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` | Fixes the build date. A change reruns the build script. | `crates/craft-log/build.rs` |
| `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR` | Set by cargo. The build script reads it to find the git folder. Tests use it through `env!` to find source and data files. | `crates/craft-log/build.rs`; `env!` in `apps/wavcraft/src/theme.rs` and `issue1173_tests.rs`, `crates/core/src/i18n.rs` (tests), `crates/testkit/tests/open.rs` |
| `CARGO_PKG_VERSION`, `CARGO_BIN_EXE_wavcraft` | Set by cargo, read through `env!`. The version goes into the log (`selflog.rs` `APP`), the About WavCraft window (`app.rs`), the session lock file (`crates/core/src/store.rs`) and MCP's `serverInfo` (`crates/automation/src/mcp.rs`). `tests/cli.rs` starts the binary from `CARGO_BIN_EXE_wavcraft`. | as listed |
| `CRAFT_LOG_TEST_CHILD`, `CRAFT_LOG_TEST_DIR`, `CRAFT_LOG_TEST_TAG`, `CRAFT_LOG_TEST_START_AT` | Child-process test harness. The children run with `CRAFT_LOG` and `RUST_LOG` removed. Some tests also set `CRAFT_LOG_JSON`, `CRAFT_LOG_STDERR=1` or a fake secret, `APP_SERVICE_TOKEN`. | `crates/craft-log/tests/process.rs` |

### Other binaries' flags

| Binary | Flags | File |
|---|---|---|
| `wavcraft-testkit` | `usage: wavcraft-testkit OUT_DIR [--long] [--huge] [--seed N] [--speech CLEAN.wav --noise NOISE.wav]`. The output folder is required, and only one. `--speech` and `--noise` go together. `-h` or `--help` prints the usage line and exits 0. Any other `--` option, a second folder, no folder or a write error prints `wavcraft-testkit: <why>` and the usage line, and exits 1. | `crates/testkit/src/main.rs` |
| `wavcraft-bench` | `FILE`, the first argument; the rest are ignored. With none: `usage: wavcraft-bench FILE`, exit 1. | `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-bench/main.rs` |
| `wavcraft-store-bench` | `FILE [--cache-mb N] [--sessions DIR] [--huge]`. The cache is 512 MB by default. Any other argument, an unknown flag included, is taken as the file; the last one wins. Errors exit 1. | `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-store-bench/main.rs` |
| `wavcraft-edit-bench` | `FILE [--cache-mb N] [--sessions DIR] [--edits N]`. Defaults: 512 MB and 200 edits. The same file rule as `wavcraft-store-bench`. Errors exit 1. | `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-edit-bench/main.rs` |
| `wavcraft-rack-bench` | `[--seconds N]`. Default 3600. A missing, non-numeric or zero value, or any other argument, prints `wavcraft-rack-bench: <why>` and exits 1. | `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-rack-bench/main.rs` |
| `wavcraft-nr-bench` | `CLEAN NOISY [--reduce-db N] [--reduction N] [--fft N] [--sessions DIR]`, or `--time [--scale N]` with the same flags and no file (G2.11): the scale is 1 by default, must be finite and at most 100, and must give at least 4 s of audio. Any other argument is taken as a file: exactly two are required, or none with `--time`. A flag without a number, a bad scale or any other count of files prints `wavcraft-nr-bench: <why>` and exits 1. | `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-nr-bench/main.rs` |
| example `spectrogram_cost` | One bare argument: minutes, clamped to 1 to 180. Missing or not a number means 180. | `crates/core/examples/spectrogram_cost.rs` |
| `tools/alpha/*.py` check scripts (134) | `--exe` (all 134), `--keep` (82), `--port` (50), `--scratch` (7: `f04`, `f30`, `f45`, `f57`, `f63`, `f72`, `f95`), `--minutes` (`f05`, `f84`), `--wav` (`f05-mem`, `g35`), `--seconds` and `--selftest` (`b1-frames`), `--scratch-root` (`f102`), `--plugins` (`f128`), `--device` (`f23`) | `tools/alpha/` |
| `tools/golden.py` | `--exe` (required), `--keep`, `--core CLONE` (also runs the wavcraft-core suites from that clone) | `tools/golden.py` |
| `tools/notices.py` | `--check` (the default) or `--write`. Anything else prints the script's help and exits 1. A disallowed licence or stale notices also exit 1. | `tools/notices.py` |
| `tools/macos/bundle.sh` | No flags. macOS only: elsewhere it exits 1. Builds the release app and writes `<target>/release/WavCraft.app`, then prints its path last. | `tools/macos/bundle.sh` |

## Deeper notes

Everything below was checked against the code at 11e49a5 and not checked again on 2026-11-16.

### Known differences from Audition

These are choices I made where Audition 26.5.0.82 was not seen. What was seen is in `docs/v1/audition-behaviour.md`.

- **One-channel Paste near the end of the file.** The clip is cut short at the end. The length never grows. Audition was only seen pasting mid-file (`paste` in `crates/core/src/doc/edit.rs`).
- **One-channel Paste over a selection.** The clip overwrites from the selection's start for the clip's length, whatever the selection's length. The step is labelled "Paste Audio".
- **Zero crossings on stereo.** An edge moves to where the sum of the enabled channels changes sign, or is exactly 0 (`zero_crossing` in `crates/core/src/actions.rs`). It looks on one side only, within 10 ms (`ZERO_CROSSING_REACH_MS`). The edge's own frame never counts, so an edge already on a crossing steps to the next one. With no crossing in reach the edge stays. An inward adjust that would empty the selection changes nothing. Audition's rule for stereo was not seen.
- **Repeat Last Command.** `repeatable` in `crates/core/src/actions.rs` allows Silence, Paste, Crop, the six zero-crossing adjusts, Reverse, Amplify, Normalize and the four fades. Insert Silence, a registered effect, DeClipper, DeClicker and the Automatic Click Remover repeat with their last settings (`Repeat`). Delete, Cut, Copy, Undo, Redo, selection moves and the view do not repeat. The keys are F3 and Shift+R. The dialog form (F2 and Ctrl+R) reopens Insert Silence or an effect with settings on the last values, and runs the others at once. `run_window_action` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs`) asks `Editor::repeat_dialog` in core (filled in by E5.1) and opens the dialog or runs the step from its answer (E5.2); it keeps no copy of the rule.
- **History labels.** One-channel Crop, Cut, Paste and Delete are labelled "Crop Audio", "Cut Audio", "Paste Audio" and "Delete Audio" (constants in `doc/edit.rs`). Those four names were checked in Audition. `every_step_name_is_the_history_label_and_the_undo_text` (`crates/core/tests/edits.rs`) pins each label. `undo_menu_and_history_agree_for_each_step` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs`) pins the Undo menu text. "Mix Paste", "Silence", "Insert Silence" and "Insert Audio from File" are my wording and are not checked against Audition.

### The store after open

Nothing holds the whole file in RAM. `Document.state` is a `DocState`, and the session store behind it is the only copy of the samples.

- **Store** (`crates/core/src/store.rs`). Samples sit in append-only pack files in a session folder. A pack rolls over at 1 GiB (`DEFAULT_PACK_BYTES`). A block never changes once written. A block is stored as `f32`, or as the 16-bit or 24-bit integers of the source, and every read hands back `f32`. The zero block is never on disk.
- **Block cache.** RAM holds only a block cache with a byte budget. The default is 1/16 of physical memory, at least 256 MiB and at most 1 GiB (`default_cache_bytes`). A miss reads one whole block with one positioned read. There is no `mmap`.
- **Lifetime.** Blocks are handed out as `Arc<BlockHandle>`. A sealed pack with no live blocks is deleted. Dropping the last handle and the last `Store` removes the session folder, unless `Store::set_keep` was called.
- **Readers.** Every consumer reads bounded chunks through `DocState::read`. `Document::audio_buffer` still reads the whole document into one `AudioBuffer`. Tests, the bench and multitrack clips use it. The waveform, the spectrogram and the player never do.
- **In-memory documents.** `Document::from_bytes` and `from_buffer` write decoded audio into a store under the system temp folder (`wavcraft-memory-sessions`). `from_bytes` is the only editor path that calls `soundcraft_audio_io::decode`.
- **Memory that still grows with the file.** The waveform peaks and the spectrogram overviews (sizes below).

### Waveform peaks

I do not use the peaks type from `soundcraft-audio-io`. `WavePeaks` in `crates/core/src/document.rs` is my own, built from chunked store reads.

- Level 0 holds one `(min, max)` pair per 256 frames (`PEAK_BLOCK`). Each further level merges 4 (`FANOUT`). A 3-hour 48 kHz channel costs about 21.6 MB.
- Level-0 blocks follow each piece's source grid, with short blocks at splices. A shifted suffix is never reread.
- Levels are kept in `Arc` chunks of 4,096 entries. A step allocates only the chunks it changes.
- `PeakBlockCache` holds level-0 summaries keyed by store block and is shared by every step of one history. It holds only weak references, so it never keeps audio alive. Its budget is 64 MiB (`BLOCK_CACHE_BYTES`).
- `Document::waveform_columns` uses the peaks when a column spans 2,048 frames or more (`PEAK_MIN_SPAN`). Closer in it reads only the frames on screen from the store and folds them itself. A failed read draws silence and logs a warning.
- The app calls `waveform_columns` while it draws, so a zoomed-in view can read a block from disk on the UI thread when the block is not in the cache.
- `Document.peak_report` says how the peaks came about: `off`, `hit`, `miss`, `stale` or `corrupt`, the time taken, and whether a step did a full rebuild.

### Spectrogram and its overview

The maths is in `crates/core/src/spectrogram.rs`. It uses `rustfft` directly.

- **Sources.** Everything takes a `spectrogram::Samples`: a slice, or `document::StoreChannel` over the store. `StoreChannel` gives silence and one log line when a read fails. The slice functions (`Overview::build`, `render`, `render_columns`) remain and give the same pictures.
- **Job.** `Job { generation, columns, rows, params }`. `Renderer::ask` sends a job only when it differs from the last one sent, with a cheap clone of the `DocState`.
- **Sizes the app asks for.** Columns are half the lane width in physical pixels, from 1 to 2,048. Rows are the lane height per channel in physical pixels, up to 768. `Columns::covering` puts the columns on a grid fixed to the file. Zoomed out, a column is a whole number of FFT hops.
- **Overview.** One per channel. For each block of 16 FFT hops (`BLOCK_HOPS`) it keeps the loudest level per bin, one byte per bin. That is about 1 byte per 16 frames: 10.8 MB per channel-hour at 48 kHz. Its levels sit in `Arc` chunks of 64 blocks, so a step shares what it does not change.
- **What an overview depends on.** FFT length, window and dB range. It does not depend on the frequency axis or the picture size. `Overview::serves` also checks the length in frames. `render_columns_from` ignores an overview that does not serve and runs every FFT itself.
- **Drawing.** A column reads whole fresh blocks from the overview and runs FFTs only for its ragged ends. `rayon` spreads columns, and an overview's chunks, across cores. The worker does channels one after another.
- **Undo straight back.** The worker re-installs the retired overview only when the piece tables match (`same_table` in `apps/wavcraft/src/spectrum.rs`).
- **The app's picture.** `SpectrumCache { job, textures }` is the newest picture, one texture per channel. The app drops it in `finish_opening` and in `set_axis` (`apps/wavcraft/src/app/spectral.rs`), among other places.

### Playback

All of this is `apps/wavcraft/src/player.rs`.

- **Device.** The chosen host and device, or the system default, with the device's default config. Sample formats f32, i16, u16 and i32. `Player::new` opens the stream at start-up. `begin_play` reopens it when there is no output or the stream failed.
- **Ring.** 2^17 frames (about 2.7 s at 48 kHz) of up to 8 channels, stored as `f32` bits in atomics. The read-ahead thread fills it in 8,192-frame chunks (`FILL_CHUNK`). The ring holds the audio in play order, not file order. The thread walks the play's `Plan` (loop, skipped part, forward or backward), so a loop wrap or a skip costs the callback nothing. A file with more than 8 channels plays its first 8.
- **Play state.** One `Mutex<Playback>` (generation, position, end, plan, direction, speed). The callback only ever uses `try_lock`. When the lock is busy it plays silence for that buffer.
- **Generations.** Every play, stop, seek, plan change and direction change starts a new generation. The callback sounds only once the thread has primed 16,384 frames (`PRIME_FRAMES`) for that generation. It writes the position back only when the generation is unchanged, so a stale callback cannot overwrite a restart.
- **Rate conversion.** `wavcraft_core::resample::Resampler` is a Kaiser windowed sinc. `fast` is 16 taps and `high` is 64 taps. The setting is `playback_quality`. The read-ahead thread builds the kernel and parks it under a mutex. The callback interpolates linearly until the kernel is there, when the kernel is wider than the ring's 1,024-frame margin, and at any speed other than 1. The playhead stays in file frames.
- **Channels.** Mono plays on both speakers. A one-channel device gets the mean of the file's channels. Files with more than two channels fold down through a fixed `Downmix` table. A `ChannelMap` can route channels to other outputs without a new stream.
- **Also in the callback.** The Effects Rack runs after the render. The UI builds a rack and parks it in a mailbox, and the callback swaps it in with `try_lock`. The metronome click and the recording monitor are mixed in here too. `Player::set_muted` silences playback while a take records.
- **Edit while playing.** `Player::replace_audio` moves the position, end and skipped part across the `EditSpan`, so the play goes on from the same place in the music.
- **Device changes.** A watcher thread checks the default output once a second. It only flags a change when the output follows the default. While the player is muted for a take, `maintain` does nothing and reopens after the take. `set_output` while muted also keeps the new choice and waits for the take to end.
- **No device.** `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO=1` runs the same callback on a `wavcraft-test-sink` thread: stereo, 480 frames at a time, 48 kHz unless `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO_RATE` says otherwise. The control verb is `PLAYBACK`.
- **Playhead.** `Player::position` returns the file frame from a snapshot of the play state. `Player::underruns` counts the dry buffers.

### What I take from the soundcraft crates

The crate-by-crate list is in the Workspace section. These are the limits and the things I do myself.

- **`soundcraft-audio-io` works on whole buffers.** `decode(bytes, ext_hint)` and `encode(&AudioBuffer, &EncodeOptions)` take and return everything in memory. `decode` refuses more than 2^30 samples in total. So I use them only where audio is in memory by design: multitrack sources and clips (`multitrack.rs`, `session_file.rs`, `mtrecord.rs`), `Document::from_bytes`, tests and the testkit.
- **Opening and saving a file in the editor are my own streaming code.** `detect_format` is the only audio-io call on the open path (`crates/core/src/io.rs`, and `crates/core/src/probe.rs`). `multitrack.rs` also calls `probe`.
- **Integer samples.** The store converts 16-bit and 24-bit blocks to `f32` with the same arithmetic audio-io uses at open, so a block reads back bit-identical.
- **`soundcraft-dsp` offline calls work on whole channels.** I call `offline::reverse` and `offline::gain` (`effects/oneshot.rs`), and `offline::time_stretch` and `offline::pitch_shift` (`timepitch.rs`).
- **Things I do not take.** The spectrogram uses `rustfft` directly. Peaks are `WavePeaks`. Playback rate conversion is `wavcraft_core::resample`. The single-file player is cpal.

## Mac, PC and Linux

### Platform-specific sites

| File | Function or item | What differs per OS |
|---|---|---|
| `crates/core/src/store.rs` | `read_exact_at` | Unix uses `FileExt::read_exact_at`. Windows loops on `seek_read`. There is no version for any other target. |
| `crates/core/src/space.rs` | `folder_free_space` | Unix asks `rustix::fs::statvfs`. Elsewhere it returns nothing and `mount_free_space` (sysinfo disks) answers. |
| `crates/core/src/save.rs` | `TempFile::create` | Unix sets mode 0600 for owner-only files. Windows inherits the folder's ACL. |
| `crates/core/src/save.rs` | `write_file_atomic` | Unix also syncs the parent folder. Windows does not. |
| `crates/core/src/atomic.rs` | `write_atomic` | The same: only unix syncs the parent folder. |
| `crates/core/src/session_file.rs` | a test of the session save | The mode 0600 check runs on unix only. |
| `crates/core/src/vst3.rs` | one test module | Built on unix only (`cfg(all(test, unix))`). |
| `crates/core/tests/sessions.rs` | one test | It has a Windows version and a version for the others. |
| `crates/core/src/autosave.rs` | `write_journal`, `sync_dir` | Unix sets mode 0600 and syncs the folder. Windows does neither. |
| `crates/core/src/settings.rs` | `path_to_native`, `path_from_native` | Paths are stored as raw bytes on unix, UTF-16LE on Windows, lossy text elsewhere. |
| `crates/core/src/settings.rs`, `store.rs`, `peakcache.rs` | `dirs::config_dir`, `cache_dir`, `data_local_dir` | Settings, presets, shortcuts, sessions and peak files live in each OS's own config and cache folders. |
| `crates/core/src/vst3.rs` | `default_dirs` | The VST3 folders come from `default_search_paths` in `soundcraft-vst3-host`, which differ per OS. |
| `crates/core/src/shortcuts.rs` | `Shortcut::text`, `menu_text`, `tooltip` | Mac shows key glyphs. The others show `Ctrl+`. The callers that pass `cfg!(target_os = "macos")` are in `app.rs` and `app/spectral.rs`. `shortcut_editor.rs` takes the flag from `app.rs`. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs` | crate attribute | Windows release builds use `windows_subsystem = "windows"` (no console). Making a subcommand's messages reach the terminal there is N2.5, not done yet. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/main.rs` | `configure_graphics` | Windows forces the DX12 backend unless `WGPU_BACKEND` is set. Mac and Linux take the wgpu default. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/control.rs` | `check_private`, `write_private` | Unix checks and sets mode bits. Every other target (`cfg(not(unix))`) calls `win_acl::others_can_read` and `win_acl::set_owner_only`. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/win_acl.rs` | whole file | Windows only. Win32 security calls through `windows-sys`. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/files.rs` | `reveal_command` | Chosen at run time from `std::env::consts::OS`, not by `cfg`. Mac runs `open -R`. Windows runs `explorer /select,`. Any other OS runs `xdg-open` on the parent folder. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/app.rs` | `reveal_label` | The menu says Finder, Explorer or Show in Folder. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/reveal.rs` | `Os`, `reveal_command` | A second copy of the reveal logic. Nothing outside its own tests calls it. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/recording.rs` | `record_dir` | Unless `WAVCRAFT_RECORD_DIR` or the `recordings_folder` setting is set, takes go to `WavCraft Recordings` in `dirs::audio_dir`, else `dirs::document_dir`, else `dirs::home_dir`. Each differs per OS. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/recording.rs` | `mic_blocked_hint`, `open_error_text` | Chosen at run time from `std::env::consts::OS` (callers: `Recording::take_hint`, from `WaveApp::logic`, and `Recording::start_with`). On a Mac only, a take that is still exact silence after two seconds, or an input that will not open, gets the note that macOS may be blocking the microphone, with the System Settings path. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/batch_panel.rs` | `BatchPanel::default` | The output folder starts as `WavCraft batch` in `dirs::audio_dir`, else the home folder. |
| `crates/craft-log/src/cli.rs` | `nonblocking` | Sets `O_NONBLOCK` on Linux only, and only on the processor types the function lists. A no-op elsewhere. |
| `crates/craft-log/src/report.rs` | `Report::new` | Linux reads `/etc/os-release` and `/proc`. Windows uses `windows_version_in_background` and `windows_processor`. Mac gets neither, so operating system, kernel and processor model read "unknown". Memory reads "unknown" too unless the app gave a reader with `set_memory_reader`. No code in this workspace outside craft-log's own tests builds a report: the app only calls `install_wrapped`, `set_log_path` and `crash::install_hook`. |
| `crates/craft-log/src/lib.rs`, `report.rs` | the build line, the architecture field | Print `std::env::consts::OS` and `ARCH`. |
| `crates/craft-log/src/report.rs` | `session` | Reads the XDG, Wayland, X11 and gamescope variables. Only Linux sets them. With none set, a Windows build answers "Windows" and the others "unknown". |
| `crates/craft-log/src/report.rs` | `find_on_path`, `first_line_of`, `kill_tree` | Windows adds `.exe` and hides the console window. Unix uses a process group and kills the group. |
| `crates/craft-log/src/report.rs` | `home_folders`, `mask_homes` | Windows masks `USERPROFILE`, `HOMEDRIVE` plus `HOMEPATH`, and `HOME`, each with both slash kinds. The others mask `HOME`. |
| `crates/testkit/src/bin/wavcraft-bench/memory.rs` | `read` | Windows calls `GetProcessMemoryInfo`. Linux reads `/proc/self/status`. Mac runs `ps -o rss=` and has no peak figure. |
| `crates/testkit/tests/edits.rs` | `long_file` | Without `WAVCRAFT_KIT` the long test looks in a hard-coded Windows folder. |
| `crates/core/Cargo.toml` | `rustix` | Unix only. |
| `apps/wavcraft/Cargo.toml`, `crates/testkit/Cargo.toml` | `windows-sys` | Windows only. |
| `apps/wavcraft/Cargo.toml` | `eframe` features `wayland`, `x11` | Only Linux uses them. |
| `tools/winlist.swift` | whole file | A Mac-only window lister for scripted checks. |

Some tests run on one OS family only. Unix only: the link test in `crates/core/tests/save.rs`, the mode check in `crates/core/tests/autosave.rs`, the non-UTF-8 folder test in `crates/core/tests/settings.rs`, two symlink tests inside `crates/core/src/settings.rs`, the read-only folder test in `crates/testkit/tests/hostile.rs`, the body of the non-Unicode argument test in `crates/craft-log/tests/process.rs`, some unit tests in `crates/craft-log/src/cli.rs` and `report.rs`, and the symlink and mode tests in `control.rs`. Windows only: the unpaired-surrogate test in `crates/core/tests/settings.rs`, one test in `crates/craft-log/src/report.rs`, and the `icacls` test in `control.rs`. Linux only: the `/proc/mounts` test in `space.rs`.

### Build prerequisites

| OS | What a build needs |
|---|---|
| All three | Rust stable 1.95 or later with rustfmt and clippy. A C compiler, because `vorbis_rs` builds libvorbis and libogg. There is no `rust-toolchain` file. |
| Linux | `libasound2-dev`, `libwayland-dev` and `pkg-config` (for cpal's ALSA binding and winit's Wayland binding). |
| Windows | The MSVC build tools supply the C compiler. Nothing else is listed in the workflow. |
| Mac | The Xcode command line tools supply the C compiler. Nothing else is listed in the workflow. |

### What CI runs

`.github/workflows/check.yml` is the only workflow. It runs on every push to `main` and on every pull request, with read-only access to the repository. A new push to a pull request cancels that pull request's older run; on `main` a new run waits for the running one instead. It has three jobs, and each job runs on all three of `ubuntu-latest`, `windows-latest` and `macos-latest` (fail-fast off, 60 minute limit). Each job installs the newest stable Rust with rustfmt and clippy (`dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable`), not the 1.95 that `rust-version` names. `clippy` and `test` cache the build with `Swatinem/rust-cache@v2`, and on Linux they first install `libasound2-dev`, `libwayland-dev` and `pkg-config` with apt. `fmt` does neither. What each job runs, and where:

| Job | Command | Linux | Windows | Mac |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fmt` | `cargo fmt --all -- --check` | yes | yes | yes |
| `clippy` | `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` | yes | yes | yes |
| `test` | `cargo test --workspace` | yes | yes | yes |

### What CI does not cover

On every OS:

- No release build and no packaging.
- No build with Rust 1.95. The jobs use the newest stable, so the `rust-version` floor is never checked, and a new clippy lint can fail `main` with no change in the code (as on 2026-11-12, fixed in `384b17f`).
- No real audio device. The tests use the `WAVCRAFT_NO_AUDIO` and fake-input paths.
- No window and no GPU. The app's drawing code never runs.
- No real VST3 plugin, no file-manager reveal, no OS clipboard.
- The `tools/alpha` scripts (`k1.py` and `l1.py` among them) and the five benches do not run.
- The long edit test in `crates/testkit/tests/edits.rs` needs the 3-hour kit file, which the runners do not have.

Per OS:

- **Linux:** the runner is a headless x86 server. Neither Wayland nor X11 is exercised. The Linux handhelds and their gamescope session are not tested.
- **Windows:** the DX12 backend choice in `configure_graphics` is never exercised. The `windows_subsystem` attribute only applies to release builds, which CI does not make.
- **Mac:** the hosted runner is not the 8 GB Mac, so memory fit there is unmeasured. The craft-log report fields for operating system, kernel and processor read "unknown" on Mac, and no test checks them.

## Planned, not on main

These belong to v1 issues that are still open on GitHub (none is labelled after-1.0). I checked each issue's state, each path with `ls` and each name with `grep` at `92d0f13`. The first column is what is still missing; the last column says what of the item is on main already.

| Planned piece | Issue | What is on main today |
|---|---|---|
| A recorded agent run of the ten checked tasks (N3.6) | #1108 | The ten tasks are built without and with the window (N3.3: `ten_tasks_headless`; N3.4: `ten_tasks_through_the_window`), and the N3.7 checker is the `ten_tasks` example. `mcp::handle` answers `initialize`, `tools/list` (seven tools: open, select, action, effect, state, save, list_effects) and `tools/call` (N3.2, built), with 10 tests in `crates/automation/tests/mcp.rs`. `wavcraft mcp` and `wavcraft mcp --attach` (N3.5) are wired in `main.rs`. No run with a real agent is recorded. |
| `crates/automation/src/script.rs` (`script::parse` N4.1 #1302, `script::run` N4.2 #1416), `History::squash_since` (N4.3, #1303), `Headless::run_script` (N4.4), the `RUN_SCRIPT` verb (N4.5), File › Run Script… (N4.7), the both-ways test (N4.6) and the hand check (N4.8) | #879 | `script::parse` and `script::run` exist (N4.1 and N4.2). `History::squash_since` exists (N4.3). `Headless::run_script` exists (N4.4) and the `RUN_SCRIPT` verb exists (N4.5: `run_script` and `ScriptBackend` in `app.rs`), and so does the both-ways test (N4.6, `five_steps_give_the_same_file_both_ways`); `apps/wavcraft/src/n4_run_script_tests.rs` has 5 tests and `crates/automation/tests/script.rs` 13. File › Run Script… (N4.7) and the hand check (N4.8) do not exist. `wavcraft run <script> <file> [-o <out>]` runs a script file on `Headless` (N2.4). |
| Subcommand messages that reach the terminal from the windowed Windows release build (N2.5) | #845 | `apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs` parses all three subcommands (N2.1), and `wavcraft convert` works with no window (N2.2), `wavcraft inspect` prints the Properties rows (N2.3), and `wavcraft run` runs a script file (N2.4). A conversion writes no log: `convert` runs before `selflog::install`, and `craft_log::cli::install` (the `CliLogger` in `crates/craft-log/src/cli.rs`) is called only by craft-log's own test `crates/craft-log/tests/process.rs`. `crates/core/src/batch_cli.rs` has no caller outside `crates/core/tests/batch_cli.rs`. |
| the Save As choices (A3.7), the ABX tool (A3.12), and the hand and by-ear checks (A3.8, A3.9) | #11 | craft-mp3 1.1.0 is pinned (A3.1, #1310), and `save_mp3` (A3.4) writes MP3 through its encoder, holding the whole stream in memory until the save ends. `crates/core/src/mp3_options.rs` (A3.2, #1311) is called by `save.rs`: `save_inner` runs `mp3_check` before any file is made, and `save_mp3` takes `Mp3Options`; with `mono` set it mixes a stereo document down to one channel (A3.5, #1464). `SaveFormat::Mp3` (A3.3, #1312) comes from a `.mp3` name. The window writes it through `save_mp3` (A3.6): `SAVE <path>.mp3 [bitrate=..] [channels=..]` works over the control line. `Headless` writes it the same way (A3.11). The Save As filters do not list MP3. Its size is estimated at constant bit rate (A3.10). |
| The model catalogue with pinned hashes (L1.7, `transcript/models.rs`), the download (L1.8), the Whisper engine on candle behind a `whisper` feature (L1.9, L1.10), the sidecar on Save and Open (L1.12), the Edit menu entry Transcribe (L1.13), the `--measure` mode of `tools/alpha/l1.py` (L1.17), and the two measurements and my read of a real transcript (L1.14 to L1.16) | #1057 (library choice: #1056, closed) | The transcript model, sidecar functions, undo step, 16 kHz windows, log-mel and the runner with `FakeEngine` are in `crates/core/src/transcript.rs` and `transcript/`. `apps/wavcraft/src/transcript_run.rs` runs it on a worker with progress and Cancel (L1.11, #1263). Without `WAVCRAFT_FAKE_SPEECH=1` a run fails with "no speech engine available". `transcript.run` has no menu entry, and the app never calls `save_beside` or `load_beside`. No speech model crate is in `Cargo.lock`. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/transcript_panel.rs`, the `transcript` panel id and the lane under the waveform (L2.2 to L2.6), a hand-labelled clip and its timing test (L2.7, L2.8), and the check on the Linux tablet (L2.9) | #1058 | None exists: `panels::REGISTRY` has no `transcript` id. A finished transcript rides on the document, but only `TRANSCRIPT state` shows it (its word count and the first word's start and end frames). |
| Edits that move the transcript (L3.3), removing `transcript_cut.rs` (L3.4), and word selection in the panel (L3.5) | #1059 | `Transcript::range_of` and `Transcript::moved_by` (L3.2, #1267) are present, core only. `finish` in `crates/core/src/doc/edit.rs` keeps the transcript only for an edit that moves no audio, and drops it otherwise. `crates/core/src/transcript_cut.rs` (`cut_ranges`) has no caller. |
| `transcript::delete_ranges` (L4.3), the `FILLERS` control verbs (L4.4), the finder in the transcript panel (L4.5), and the bench and its run on the Linux tablet (L4.6, L4.7) | #1060 | The finders are present, core only (`crates/core/src/transcript/filler.rs`: `fillers`, `pauses`, `default_pause_frames`, `shorten_cut`). No `delete_ranges`, no panel and no control verb. |
| The SRT writer (L5.3), the find and export actions (L5.4), the search box (L5.5), the export dialogs (L5.6) and the playback check on the Linux tablet (L5.7) | #1061 | `transcript/search.rs` (`find`, `next_match`, `prev_match`) and `transcript/export.rs` (`plain_text`) are present, core only. No SRT writer, no `transcript.find` action, and Ctrl+F is not bound. |
| Effects › Run Episode Check and a toolbar button for it (K1.7), and the bench run on the Linux tablet (K1.9) | #1049 | The runner is on main (K1.6, #1255): `CheckRun` in `apps/wavcraft/src/check_panel.rs`, started by `run_action` in `app.rs`, with progress, Cancel and a stale note in the status bar, and the control verbs `CHECK run`, `cancel` and `state`. The Effects menu draws its action rows from `effect.` actions only (`v1::effect_rows_in`), then Effects Rack… and, in a debug build, the Diagnostics entries. So `check.run` has no menu entry, no toolbar button and no default key. It runs from the control channel, or from a key set in Edit › Keyboard Shortcuts…. The bench is `crates/core/tests/episode_check_bench.rs` (ignored); there is no `docs/checklists/K1.md`. |
| The findings panel: choosing a finding (K3.3), next and previous (K3.4), the rows (K3.5), pins on the map (K3.7) and the look in the running app on the Linux tablet (K3.8) | #1053 | The issue puts the panel in a new `apps/wavcraft/src/findings_panel.rs`, which is absent. K3.5 in the roadmap puts the rows in `check_panel.rs` instead: `findings_rows` draws one 28 pt row per finding, with the highlight on the current one and a click returning its index. `panel_body` in `app.rs` has the `findings` arm, which calls it inside a vertical ScrollArea and sends `CHECK click <row>` on a click. K3.2 is on main (#1258): `findings` (title Episode Check, right dock) is in `panels::REGISTRY`, and `check.next` (F8) and `check.prev` (Shift+F8) are in the action and shortcut tables. The window still does nothing for `check.next` or `check.prev` (K3.4). The list logic is in `crates/core/src/check/list.rs`; `CHECK list` and `CHECK click` are in `control_request`. |
| Applying a suggested fix (K4.4 to K4.6), the Fix button and `CHECK fix` (K4.8), Before/After (K4.9), and the listening check on the Linux tablet (K4.10) | #1054 | `check::fix::suggest` (`crates/core/src/check/fix.rs`) is present, core only, and gives no fix for Clipping or True peak yet. There is no `check::fix::apply`. The K4.2 answer is written down (`docs/v1/questions-and-answers.md`, Q10: DeClipper for Clipping, the Dynamics limiter for True peak). |
| The test against `loudness::measure` (K5.2), the summary in the findings panel and `CHECK summary` (K5.3), its MP3 line (K5.4) and the comparison in the running app (K5.5) | #1055 | `crates/core/src/check/summary.rs` (`Summary`, `Summary::new`, `target_lufs`; K5.1, #1240) is present, core only. Nothing in `apps/` uses it. |
| `apps/wavcraft/src/faces.rs` with the four Podcast steps (M1.2), the `face` setting (M1.1), the `FACE` control verb (M1.3), the View menu entries and toolbar switch (M1.4), the step bar with `STEP` and `STEPTOOL` (M1.5), the Podcast layout `faces::panels_shown` (M1.6), the tools of each step (M1.7 to M1.11) and the hand check on the Mac (M1.12) | #1063 | None exists. |
| The Episode Map across the top, with marker ticks (M2.5) and finding pins (M2.7), in both faces (M2.8), and its timing on a 3-hour file (M2.6) | #1052 | `crates/core/src/episode_map.rs` (`Class`, `classify`, `columns`, `pin_x`, `span_x`) is present, and `WaveApp::navigator` paints each column in its class colour (M2.4). `EPISODEMAP` reports the columns. Nothing outside `crates/core/tests/m2_episode_map.rs` calls `episode_map::pin_x` or `span_x`. |
| Large controls: `apply_look` calling `theme::set_large` (M4.3), the Preferences checkbox (M4.4), the navigator grab (M4.5), the Podcast button size (M4.6) and the touch check on the Linux handheld (M4.7) | #1062 | Partly built. The `large_controls` key is in `crates/core/src/settings.rs`, and `metrics_for`, `set_large` and `metrics` are in `apps/wavcraft/src/theme.rs`. The marker flag grab (`handle_at` in `markers_panel.rs`) already reads `theme::metrics().handle_grab`. But `apply_look` does not call `set_large`, Preferences has no checkbox, and the navigator in `app.rs` still passes a fixed 6.0 to `nav_hit`. |
| Hiding the last out-of-v1 entries in release builds: the label list `menus::bar_labels` (M5.5) and the hand check of a release build (M5.6) | #1051 | Partly built. `apps/wavcraft/src/v1.rs` hides the menus and Tools entries of seven features (multitrack, CD layout, video, batch, Mix Paste, diagnostics, VST3 plugins) in release builds. The Keyboard Shortcuts list and the Effects Rack leave out the Diagnostics commands and DeHummer and DeReverb (`effects::OUT_OF_V1`), and the Effects menu draws its rows from `v1::effect_rows_in` (M5.2). `PanelSet::for_build` (`crates/core/src/panels.rs`) hides the Frequency Analysis panel (`panels::OUT_OF_V1`). The Window › Mixer panel checkbox and the panel are drawn only when `v1::offered(Feature::Multitrack)` (M5.4). The Ogg Vorbis Save As filter is hidden in release builds too (M5.3): `save_with` takes its filter list from `v1::save_formats_in(v1::RELEASE, first)`, which leaves Ogg Vorbis out of a release build's list. Both the Mixer checkbox and the Ogg filter are done. |

These pieces are on main now, so they left the table (their rows are in Files by area): `apps/wavcraft/src/cli.rs` and `wavcraft convert` (N2.1 #1298, N2.2 #1299), the window's use of `wavcraft-automation` (N1.6, #1252), and the Episode Check runner `apps/wavcraft/src/check_panel.rs` (K1.6, #1255). Their issues stay open for the steps in the rows above.
